## Note CE — GUI layout v2, branch 4 (main team, 2026-10-18)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC9, 2026-10-29)
- **CE2 The Enter default is filled, destructive is outlined (A3).** The Default is filled with `visuals.selection.bg_fill`; a Destructive button gets a 1.5 px outline in `status::RED`. Teal now means "selection and the one primary action" (the header Export, the start screen's Open folder… and Review's Export already did). Red is the existing "blocks export" colour. A dialog where Enter does nothing has no filled button. (superseded by FC9)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC5, 2026-10-29)
- **CE3 Renamed buttons, one line per dialog.** (superseded by FC5)
  - Close / Open unsaved: Cancel, Discard (destructive), Save (Enter). Body "Your edits are not saved." with a weak hint "sdc.toml, sdc-project.toml".
  - Reload with unsaved edits: Discard and reload (destructive), Cancel (Enter).
  - Export running: Stop the export and close, or Stop the export and open the folder (destructive), Keep exporting (Enter).
  - Changed on disk: Reload from disk, Overwrite with my changes (destructive), Cancel (Enter).
  - PGPatcher JSON edited: Overwrite {name} (destructive), Keep the edited {name} (Enter).
  - Error: OK (Enter). Reset all: Reset (destructive), Cancel (Enter). Delete set: Delete (destructive), Cancel (Enter). Set-drop Refused: OK (Enter), titled "Can't do that".
  - Fill from names: Cancel, Fill (Enter). Copy settings: Cancel, Apply, both plain. Discard changes? (preset creator): Cancel, Discard (destructive), no Default.
  - Set rename ("Rename {id}"): Cancel, OK, both plain (the field's own Enter renames).
  - Rename dialog ("Rename {n} inputs", "Rename outputs of {n} sets"): Cancel, Apply (Enter, filled while it is enabled; CE8).

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FB7, 2026-10-29)
- **CE11 Presets placement (A9, R19, K6, K7).** The preset creator opens below the header and over the centre column. `app.rs` records `below-header` (the rectangle before the bottom panel, after the scan row) and `centre-rect` (the central panel; in the Meshes view both are the same rectangle). `presets::place` pulls a saved rectangle below the header and into the area; the window also gets `constrain_to(area)` and `max_size(area.size())` every frame, because `constrain_to` alone lets an oversize window rise over the header. The JSON editor (an OS viewport), the Plugin refs window, the 3D settings window and the `meshes` / `viewport3d` modals are not changed (R19, NIF files).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC8, 2026-10-29)
- **CE16 The preview's settings collapse into a strip (A6, A10, A12).** `preview-settings` shows while it fits beside a `PREVIEW_MIN` picture or once opened; otherwise `preview-settings-strip` (a separate id, `resizable(false)`, 24 px, as `advanced-strip`) holds a **Settings** button. The saved `preview-settings` width is never written by the strip. Once opened, the settings stay open until the preview closes, including a direct switch to another texture (`PreviewUi` resets only when nothing is expanded). At the default 1400×900 window the centre is 736 px, so the settings start collapsed there and at 1280×800; at 1600 they show, and opening Advanced there collapses them again. (superseded by FC8)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CE13 The toolbar repaints once when it gains a row.** `Panel::top` clips to last frame's height, so on the frame a row appears (the first draw, or a pin adding Compare and Unpin) the new row is drawn clipped. `preview::draw` calls `request_repaint()` when the stored height changed; a click that grows the toolbar is never run twice (`request_repaint`, not `request_discard`). One clipped frame can flash.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CE14 Encode now is filled only while it can encode (A9).** "The one filled button" means the one filled push button. Selected toggles (RGBA, Side by side, sRGB, Lens, …) keep egui's selected fill. The "Not encoded" pane's own Encode now button is unchanged.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CE18 Shots.** Re-shot in `.superpowers/ui-shots-after-4/` (not committed): every dialog, Presets, and the preview toolbar at 1600, 1280 and 1100 with Advanced open and closed, plus pinned.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CE21 A clamped window never overwrites its saved size (final review I3).** egui writes a size clamp into a window's own resize state, so `layout::capture` saved the clamped Presets rect and a saved 860×680 came back 604 tall at the next start after one session in a 1100×700 window. `layout::window_rect` keeps the saved width or height when the drawn one is at the clamp (within 1 pt of the area: below the header for Presets, the window for the 3D settings window), still takes the position, and saves nothing for a first rect that is clamped. `shortcut:` a user who drags a window to the full height keeps their previous height. Not fixed here: a saved W×H opens as (W+3)×(H+3) and that is saved back, so the size creeps by 3 points per start (measured at 1600×1000; it predates this branch).

## Note CF — GUI motion and per-frame cost (main team, 2026-10-18)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CF5, 2026-10-19)
- **CF7 The Sets column lays out only the cards on screen; a set drag is dropped on the target card (owner decision c, R1, R4).** The changes the user can see are listed in the last item.
  - Virtualisation (R4) is a clip-rect skip in `sets_column::draw`, not `show_rows`, because cards differ in height (the narrow layout, the shader combo only on a set with a key, chip rows that wrap). Each card keeps its last laid-out height in `DropState::card_h` (by set id); a card never laid out uses `CARD_EST = 110` pt until any card is measured, then the mean. A card whose estimated rect is outside the clip rect only calls `ui.allocate_space` for that height (one auto id, the same as the card's `scope_builder`, so later cards keep their ids, context menus and drop targets; preflight K9). When a drawn card's height differs from the one used by more than 0.5 pt, one `request_repaint` lays the cards below it out again; it settles once they are measured. A reveal (Go to set, Move to…, any reorder through `apply_reorder`) of a card outside last frame's view jumps to the offset the known heights give, the least scroll that shows it (review I1: aiming `scroll_to_rect` once at an estimated rect landed tens of cards off with mixed heights). It aims again each frame, up to `REVEAL_TRIES = 8`, until the card is laid out with no height changing that frame. A card in or across the view still glides there with `scroll_to_rect`. Known limit (a `shortcut:` comment): heights of off-screen cards go stale when the column width changes, so the scroll bar length is off until they are drawn.
  - Drag-reorder stays (R1). The drop is now read on the card under the pointer with `Response::dnd_release_payload::<DragSet>()`, only while a set hovers that card (a stray take would eat a dragged texture, K9), and goes through `reorder_target` and `apply_reorder` as before. The ≡ handle still senses drag and sets the payload but no longer reads `drag_stopped()`, which never fires once its own card is skipped; egui keeps the payload while the source widget is gone and clears it at the end of the release pass, so a release outside any card moves nothing.
  - The limit the user can see is unchanged: egui 0.36.2 ignores the mouse wheel while a widget is dragged (`scroll_area.rs:1215-1217`) and has no edge auto-scroll, so a drag reaches only the cards on screen. Move to… (CF5) is the way to make a long move. Task 10 says so in docs/gui.md.
  - Tests (`set_order`): `a_reveal_lands_on_a_far_card_among_mixed_heights` (20 short cards first, the rest taller; RED landed on card 341 of the wanted 450), `the_sets_column_lays_out_only_the_cards_on_screen` (500 sets, `set_cards_drawn` counts `pill` calls; `run` settling within 16 steps is the settle pin, preflight K2), `a_palette_pick_scrolls_an_undrawn_set_into_view`, `a_drag_released_after_the_source_scrolled_away_still_reorders` (Review Focus 1; the content is shifted with `nav.reveal_set` because the wheel is off during a drag, K7), and the guards `the_wheel_does_not_scroll_the_sets_during_a_drag` and `a_drop_outside_any_card_moves_nothing`. Fixture: `support::many_sets`. `the_number_slot_has_one_width_for_one_and_two_digits` now uses a window tall enough to show all 12 cards, since cards off screen have no number label any more.
  - What the user can see changes (review I2):
    - Screen readers and Tab reach only the cards on screen: an off-screen card has no AccessKit node and is not in the Tab order, Tab from the last visible card leaves the column, and a focused card that scrolls off loses focus. Ctrl+K Go to set and Move to… still reach every set, and each card's label still says "of N".
    - A context menu or shader combo open on a card goes away while that card is scrolled out of view (neither is drawn for a skipped card).
    - The scroll thumb's length changes as card heights are learned. After a jump (a thumb drag or a far reveal), scrolling back up shifts the visible cards as each card above is measured (a `shortcut:` comment names the fix: anchor the top visible card).
    - A far reveal now jumps instead of gliding (above).
    - A release on a card that was not laid out on the previous frame is ignored and moves nothing (egui hit-tests against the previous frame's rects). It shows only when the content moves on the release frame, for example when a plan lands.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC9, 2026-10-29)
- **CF18 Shots (Task 10).** `.superpowers/ui-shots-after-motion/` (untracked, with an `INDEX.md`): 31 images at 1600x1000, 1280x800 and 1100x800 from the real `SdcApp` under egui_kittest with wgpu (step 0.05 s of egui time). Sets cards with numbers, unfiltered and filtered; Move to… with "1", "99" and a name; 500 sets scrolled to set 251 (`support::many_sets`); Advanced > Interface; Review with Export ready; two toasts; and the slot editor at rest, 0.1 s after a change and in the fade. The capture test (`crates/gui/tests/ui_shots.rs`) is not committed. Checked against ui-rules groups 2 and 3: panels, widths and truncation hold at the three widths; nothing overlaps; the Move to… dialog, the toasts and the Review footer fit. Observations: (superseded by FC9)
  - With Advanced open at 1100 the Sets column is about 110 pt wide and names, the shader combo and the *Needs attention* pill truncate. That layout is unchanged by this branch.
  - Shot 31 shows no slot flash. The Normal row's blue outline there is the RNM outline (90,150,230, `rnm_section::RNM_OUTLINE`): steel_n now blends the dropped bump layer, and no slot got a member, so nothing flashed (final review 4b and M5; the flash is the accent, 192,222,255).
  - Motion cannot be judged from stills. Look at the toast, the Export accent, the press scale and the folder fade-in in the running release build, with `Style::animation_time` raised temporarily if needed (animate "Output").

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF1 Reduce motion (R10).** One checkbox, **Reduce motion**, in a new **Interface** section of the Advanced drawer, saved in `gui.toml` as `reduce_motion` (default off, so motion is on). `ui::draw_with_scan` mirrors it into an egui context flag every frame (`ui::motion`), so a painter reads it without the model. One rule for every animation this branch adds, and for the toast fade: movement (offset, scale, the progress glide, the press scale) takes 0 s through `motion::move_dur`; fades and colour changes are capped at `motion::FADE_CAP` = 0.12 s through `motion::fade_dur`. egui's stock motion (`Area` fades, collapsing headers, `Style::animation_time`) is not touched.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF2 Toasts (R6).** The whole toast fades: `multiply_opacity` sits in a `ui.scope` around `Frame::popup`, so the frame and its shadow fade with the text (before, only the text faded and the solid box then vanished in one frame). A new toast fades in over 0.2 s (`emath::easing::quadratic_out`) and rises 8 pt to its place over the same entry; the rise runs only until the toast first reaches full opacity. The exit is a 0.2 s `quadratic_out` fade with no movement, starting when the toast's time left is at most the fade (`model::messages::FADE_OUT`; it was 500 ms). One `animate_bool_with_time_and_easing` per toast (`ui::messages::toast_anim_id`), seeded closed on the frame a toast is first seen. A toast starts exiting when `left <= fade` and keeps exiting until it is gone (`exiting = !hovered && (left <= fade || was exiting)`, target `!exiting`), so turning on Reduce motion mid-exit, which shrinks `fade`, never brightens it again; only a hover reverses an exit. The motion runs on egui time while the toast's life stays on the model clock, so a hovered toast (whose clock stops) still finishes its entry, and a toast hovered during its exit fades back up (the snap on that reversal is accepted). The toasts `Area` no longer fades in on its own (`fade_in(false)`), so the first toast doesn't fade twice. `Toast::opacity()` is removed. Reduce motion: no rise, and both fades are capped at 0.12 s. Repaint: the 50 ms poll for a toast's whole life is gone. A resting toast asks for one frame when its fade is due (`request_repaint_after` of the smallest time to a fade or, inside one, to removal), and the animate call repaints only while something moves. A hovered toast asks for no timer; its time stops from the last frame it was hovered (final review I2: `Messages` keeps the last tick's hovered set, so the frame a resting pointer leaves on, whose `dt` is the whole rest, charges it nothing). Tests: `messages::a_toast_rested_on_with_no_frames_keeps_its_time_after_the_pointer_leaves` and `a_hovered_toast_near_its_fade_asks_for_no_short_timer`. Accepted (preflight R-c): a pointer that leaves a toast inside its last 0.2 s lets the model remove it before the egui fade ends, a small pop at low alpha. Known limit (`shortcut:` in `ui/messages.rs`): an anchored `Area` places itself from last frame's size, so on a new toast's first visible frame the whole stack draws one toast-height low for one frame. Not fixed with `request_discard`, because a discarded pass can handle a click twice. The tests measure the rise against the other toast in the stack.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF3 Keyboard-opened overlays appear at once (spec 01#2, Do first 4 and 11).** The Ctrl+K palette, the F1 shortcut sheet and the path autocomplete list no longer fade in: egui's 0.2 s `Area` fade made a key press wait for the overlay. `dialogs::modal_keys` takes a `fade_in` flag (the palette and the sheet pass `false`, building the `Modal` on `Modal::default_area(id).fade_in(false)`); the autocomplete `Area` in `ui/input_name.rs` sets `.fade_in(false)`. Mouse-opened dialogs keep egui's stock 0.2 s fade: `dialogs::dialog` passes `true`, and the few direct `egui::Modal::new` calls (`ui/meshes.rs`, `ui/viewport3d.rs`) use the stock area. Reduce motion does not touch either (stock motion is left alone, CF1). Tests: `keyboard::the_palette_and_the_sheet_open_without_a_fade` and `input_name_edit::the_path_list_shows_at_once_when_typing_reopens_it` compare the overlay's alpha on its first visible frame with its resting alpha (a repaint pin cannot tell, because any new `Area` asks for one repaint); the error dialog is the control that does fade. kittest zeroes `Style::animation_time`, so those tests set it back to 0.2.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF5 Move to… (owner decision b, R3).** A set's context menu has **Move to…** after Move up / Move down. It closes the menu and opens a dialog (`Pending::MoveTo`, `dialogs::dialog(.., "set-move-to", false, 420.0, "Move {id} to…", [Cancel])`), not a submenu or dropdown: the owner ruled that a dialog is easier to type in (2026-10-19), and it avoids the one-popup trap (CD21). It keeps egui's stock fade because a mouse menu opens it (CF3). The field (hint "Number or name", accessible name "Position or set name") takes the focus. Up and Down move the highlight (consumed before the field, with the palette's focus-lock filter). The list is `show_rows`, up to 240 pt; each row reads "{n}  {label}", with a weak "(hidden by filter)" for a set the filter hides. The rules (`sets_column::move_to_hits`): a blank query lists every set. A query that is all digits after trimming is a position only and never matches a name (controller ruling on review I1, which overrides the brief's "then the name matches"): `n` in 1..=N lists that one row; a number outside 1..=N (or too big to parse) lists no row and shows "Positions are 1 to N". Otherwise, "13" with 12 sets would have moved the set to one named "armor_13". A set named with digits only is found by typing a letter of its folder or label. Any other text lists the sets whose folded id or label holds the folded query (folded like the Sets search), in the full order. The highlight resets to the first row when the query changes. Enter (the field's own, so never on the opening frame) or a click picks the highlighted row. Enter with no row does nothing and the dialog stays open, saying "Positions are 1 to N" for an out-of-range number or "Nothing matches" for text, so a miss always says why. The pick is the final 0-based index for `reorder_set`, so moving down takes the target's place and the target shifts up one. One case is silent: picking the set's own position closes the dialog and changes nothing, with no message (`reorder_set` refuses it: no step, not dirty). Esc and Cancel close; a click outside does not (it holds typed text). Every reorder (drag, Move up / Move down, Move to…) now goes through `apply_reorder` (preflight K5: `pending_modal` runs after the column's act loop). The moved set flashes as before, and `nav.reveal_set` is set to its key when it has one, so a far move scrolls into view. That scroll glides with egui's stock scroll animation, and lands at once under Reduce motion (`scroll_to_rect_animation` with `ScrollAnimation::none()`; final review D2: these reorder triggers are new, so CF1's rule covers them; test `set_order::a_reveal_glide_lands_at_once_with_reduce_motion`). The reveal path is shared, so Go to set (Ctrl+K, Problems) to a card in or near view also lands at once under Reduce motion; this supersedes the CF7 wording that a card in view always glides. A move never changes the selection, the filter or the open set. On a read-only project the entry is disabled with the banner's reason, like the other entries. Tests: `set_order` (`move_to_*`, `a_number_past_the_end_never_picks_a_set_named_with_it`, `the_number_slot_has_one_width_for_one_and_two_digits`, `esc_closes_move_to_with_no_change`, `ctrl_z_does_nothing_while_move_to_is_open`) and `sets_column::tests::move_to_hits_reads_numbers_then_names`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF10 Hover and press looks (Task 7).** Instant, no animation, so no reduce-motion branch (CF1 has nothing to switch). `columns::interact_stroke` gives tiles and unselected member chips `widgets.active.bg_stroke` while pressed and `widgets.hovered.bg_stroke` while hovered; an AMBER or RED status stroke always wins; selected chips keep the selection stroke. A pressed strip fills with `widgets.active.weak_bg_fill` under its text. The dimmed drag source gets no press stroke, so it reads as dimmed rather than lit.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF11 Export fades to the accent (Task 8).** When Export becomes available, the Review footer button and the header button fill fade from `widgets.inactive.weak_bg_fill` to `selection.bg_fill`, and the header dot fades in as `GREEN.gamma_multiply(t)`. One `animate_bool_with_time_and_easing` per paint (`review-export-ready`, `header-export-ready`, `header-export-dot`), `cubic_out`, `motion::fade_dur(0.15)` (so 0.12 s under reduce motion, CF1). Losing readiness snaps (0 s), and the call runs every frame, also while the gate is `Err`, so an untick resets the state to 0 and a re-tick fades again; the unready button keeps the disabled look. Only paint animates: the button is enabled and clickable on the frame the gate passes, the red reason vanishes at once, and the readiness inputs (`review_gate().is_ok()`, `can_export()`, `export_dot()`) and the click path are unchanged (ui-rules 7.1-7.2). Enter does nothing in Review (N3). Tests: `review_tab::export_fades_to_the_accent_and_snaps_back` (the fill is strictly between after one step, the accent after four more, the disabled fill again after the untick) and `reduced_motion_brings_the_export_accent_sooner_than_the_control` (one step in, against an unreduced control; final review M2). `the_review_export_works_on_the_frame_it_becomes_ready` is a guard, green before the change. The tests tick with `click()` plus `step()`, not `press()`, because `press()` runs the fade to its end.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF12 Progress glide (Task 9).** The header's export bar and the Files tab's bar draw `ui::header::shown_progress(ctx, model.drawer_epoch, frac)`: `animate_value_with_time(Id::new(("export-progress", drawer_epoch)), frac, motion::move_dur(0.3))`, linear, so each step glides 0.3 s from the value on screen (0 s, today's jump, under reduce motion). The bar text stays the exact `done/total`, and `.animate(false)` stays. A new export bumps `drawer_epoch`, a new animation id whose first call returns its target, so a second run never sweeps back from the last run's value. egui returns the old value on the frame the target changes, so the glide starts one frame after the count. Test: `plan_progress::the_export_bar_glides_and_a_new_run_starts_at_its_own_value`.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF13 Reorder flash timing (Task 9; preflight K13).** A moved set's border now holds full for 0.35 s (`sets_column::FLASH_HOLD`), then fades over 0.25 s (`FLASH_FADE`) with alpha `(1 - k)²`; it was a 1 s linear fade (`FLASH_SECS`, removed). `PillLook::flash` is the alpha. `sets_column::flash_now` asks for one frame at the end of the hold (`request_repaint_after_secs`) and for every frame only during the fade, so a flash costs about 15 frames, not 60. Reduce motion keeps the hold and shortens the fade to 0.12 s (`fade_dur`). A newer reorder replaces the flash. Test: `sets_column::tests::flash_alpha_holds_then_eases_out`.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF14 Slot flash (Task 9; R9).** In the set editor, a slot whose member changed since the last frame the editor drew, in the same set, and now holds a member flashes its row with a 2 px accent (`selection.stroke.color`) outline on the same timing as CF13. The outline is `StrokeKind::Inside` (final review M1): `Outside` lost the first row's top edge to the slot `ScrollArea`'s clip. `set_editor::slot_changes` compares each slot's member key, by slot number, with `DropState::slot_seen`, so every way a slot changes flashes: a drop, Replace, Assign…, an Explorer file drop, and undo/redo (undo flashing the slot it restores is intended). A slot that only empties (a texture moved to another slot, an undone drop) does not flash, so a move from slot 1 to slot 5 flashes slot 5 (review M1). Only the first slot that got a member flashes; a newer change replaces it. Drawing another set (a switch) only records its slots and never flashes. Wherever the set editor is not drawn (another selection, a texture expanded, the set gone from the plan), `slot_seen` is cleared, so a change made meanwhile does not flash when the set shows again (review M2). Known limit: slots are keyed by slot number, and two kinds share some numbers (Emissive / Skin 3, Coat normal / Fuzz 7); with both filled, the last kind in slot order is compared, so a change to the other may not flash. Tests: `set_editor_ui::a_slot_that_gets_a_texture_flashes_and_a_set_switch_does_not` (also pins K13: a timer during the hold, an immediate frame during the fade), `undoing_a_drop_does_not_flash_the_emptied_slot_and_redo_does` and `a_slot_changed_while_the_editor_was_hidden_does_not_flash`.

The hit rect keeps its 56 pt. The brief's `with_visual_transform` is not used: its scope allocates a cell in the slot Grid and would shift the row's columns, so the painted rect is scaled instead, which is the same transform for these shapes (the 1 px border and 2 pt corners shrink by 3% too). A quick tap reverses mid-way; the up-to-1-pt jump at 0.97 is accepted (02#6). Reduce motion: no scale. Test: `set_editor_ui::a_pressed_slot_thumbnail_scales_to_097`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CF7 The Sets column lays out only the cards on screen; a set drag is dropped on the target card (owner decision c, R1, R4).** The changes the user can see are listed in the last item.
  - Virtualisation (R4) is a clip-rect skip in `sets_column::draw`, not `show_rows`, because cards differ in height (the narrow layout, the shader combo only on a set with a key, chip rows that wrap). Each card keeps its last laid-out height in `DropState::card_h` (by set id); a card never laid out uses `CARD_EST = 110` pt until any card is measured, then the mean. A card whose estimated rect is outside the clip rect only calls `ui.allocate_space` for that height (one auto id, the same as the card's `scope_builder`, so later cards keep their ids, context menus and drop targets; preflight K9). When a drawn card's height differs from the one used by more than 0.5 pt, one `request_repaint` lays the cards below it out again; it settles once they are measured. A reveal (Go to set, Move to…, any reorder through `apply_reorder`) of a card outside last frame's view jumps to the offset the known heights give, the least scroll that shows it (review I1: aiming `scroll_to_rect` once at an estimated rect landed tens of cards off with mixed heights). It aims again each frame, up to `REVEAL_TRIES = 8`, until the card is laid out with no height changing that frame. A card in or across the view is scrolled to with `scroll_to_rect` and lands at once (Note FM25). Known limit (a `shortcut:` comment): heights of off-screen cards go stale when the column width changes, so the scroll bar length is off until they are drawn.
  - Drag-reorder stays (R1). The drop is now read on the card under the pointer with `Response::dnd_release_payload::<DragSet>()`, only while a set hovers that card (a stray take would eat a dragged texture, K9), and goes through `reorder_target` and `apply_reorder` as before. The ≡ handle still senses drag and sets the payload but no longer reads `drag_stopped()`, which never fires once its own card is skipped; egui keeps the payload while the source widget is gone and clears it at the end of the release pass, so a release outside any card moves nothing.
  - The limit the user can see is unchanged: egui 0.36.2 ignores the mouse wheel while a widget is dragged (`scroll_area.rs:1215-1217`) and has no edge auto-scroll, so a drag reaches only the cards on screen. Move to… (CF5) is the way to make a long move. Task 10 says so in docs/gui.md.
  - Tests (`set_order`): `a_reveal_lands_on_a_far_card_among_mixed_heights` (20 short cards first, the rest taller; RED landed on card 341 of the wanted 450), `the_sets_column_lays_out_only_the_cards_on_screen` (500 sets, `set_cards_drawn` counts `pill` calls; `run` settling within 16 steps is the settle pin, preflight K2), `a_palette_pick_scrolls_an_undrawn_set_into_view`, `a_drag_released_after_the_source_scrolled_away_still_reorders` (Review Focus 1; the content is shifted with `nav.reveal_set` because the wheel is off during a drag, K7), and the guards `the_wheel_does_not_scroll_the_sets_during_a_drag` and `a_drop_outside_any_card_moves_nothing`. Fixture: `support::many_sets`. `the_number_slot_has_one_width_for_one_and_two_digits` now uses a window tall enough to show all 12 cards, since cards off screen have no number label any more.
  - What the user can see changes (review I2):
    - Screen readers and Tab reach only the cards on screen: an off-screen card has no AccessKit node and is not in the Tab order, Tab from the last visible card leaves the column, and a focused card that scrolls off loses focus. Ctrl+K Go to set and Move to… still reach every set, and each card's label still says "of N".
    - A context menu or shader combo open on a card goes away while that card is scrolled out of view (neither is drawn for a skipped card).
    - The scroll thumb's length changes as card heights are learned. After a jump (a thumb drag or a far reveal), scrolling back up shifts the visible cards as each card above is measured (a `shortcut:` comment names the fix: anchor the top visible card).
    - A reveal lands at once; none glides.
    - A release on a card that was not laid out on the previous frame is ignored and moves nothing (egui hit-tests against the previous frame's rects). It shows only when the content moves on the release frame, for example when a plan lands.

## Note CL — v2 artist checks (owner, 2026-10-20)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC13, 2026-10-29)
- **CL2 Hints by canonical suffix.** `checks::hints_for(suffix, cache.checks)`: bands and luma for every texture; curl for canonical `n` (every vocabulary normal, `normal`, `nrm`, `norm`, `normalgl`, `normaldx`, canonicalises to `n`); steps for `p` (R) and `m` (A, also VanillaM: a harmless extra pass). Pass 1 takes the suffix from the file's split (now computed once, before the cache lookup); pass 2 from `RecipeKind::suffix()`. Retyping a file does not re-measure it: the file cache is keyed by path and stamp, not by hints (`shortcut:` comment in `hints_for`). (superseded by FC13)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC3, 2026-10-29)
- **CL5 Artist check warnings (GUI, Task 2).** Placement under layout v2 (Q1, owner answers 1-2, 2026-10-19; Checks filter and amber list dots, owner 2026-10-20). (superseded by FC3)
  - `model::checks::texture_checks` turns one texture's cached stats (`TextureItem::analysis`, filled in `PlanView::build` from `AnalysisCache::analysis`; `None` for skipped, failed and packed-source items) into `CHK-*` warnings; `refresh_checks` adds dark metal per set and the landscape scale outliers. The results live only in `AppModel`'s check maps (`check_warnings(item)`, `set_check_warnings(set)`), never in `TextureItem::warnings` or `SetView::set_warnings`, so `tile_ready` and the card's "⚠ n" never see them.
  - Warning-level results are Problems rows at `bottom::Level::Check` (after `Warning`). `counts_as_ready_problem` is false for them, and `flagged` inserts only rows that pass it, so the set and card dots, the Sets filter's "Needs attention" and the header Export dot ignore checks; the tile dots read `tile_ready` (`item.warnings`), which never holds a check result. `Flagged` also counts `ready_rows` (the Export dot and its "M problems" hover) and `check_rows` (the Problems tab's "Checks {n}"), so neither scans the list per frame (preflight K6). Review's "N other warnings" still counts `Level::Warning` only.
  - Info-level results (`CHK-GREEN-GUESS`) are never rows: they show on the card badge's hover (and, in Task 3, in the texture's own list).
  - Each Check row's own dot in the Problems list is amber (`status_of(Level::Check) = Warning`): the list's severity marker, not a "ready" dot.
  - The Problems tab shows "Checks {n}" while check rows exist; it sets `ProblemsFilter::Checks`, whose chip reads "Checks only ×" and clears it. The filter is part of `ui::problems::rows`' existing cache key (preflight A2).
  - The card's "ⓘ n" badge (`ui::sets_column::check_badge`) sits beside "⚠ n" and wraps with it on a narrow card (CD9). `n` counts the members' and the set's Warning-level results; it is plain "ⓘ" when only Info results exist; the text is the weak text colour, not `AMBER`; the hover (lazy, `on_hover_ui`) lists every result; a click opens Problems on the set; its AccessKit name is "Check results: {n}" (preflight K4).
  - Engine `CHK-*` codes on export rows (Task 4's CHK-LOW-DETAIL) become `Level::Check` rows in the File-row loop and are skipped while their switch is off (preflight K2). The switches are not in `ProblemsKey`: a switch change reaches the build through `refresh_checks`' `invalidate_views()`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC3, 2026-10-29)
- **CL7 Codes and severities.** `CHK-RANGE`, `CHK-PLASTIC`, `CHK-Z-SIGN`, `CHK-PARALLAX` and `CHK-PARALLAX-SCALE` are Warning; `CHK-GREEN-GUESS` is Info. Plastic is a Warning with soft text ("may look plastic"; spec: soft by design, mesh spec values vary); the green guess is Info because the curl test is weak on straight ridges and never auto-flips (spec C3, Note AW). Every `CHK-RANGE` carries `{"rule": id}` (`albedo_luma`, `metal_grey`, `rough_extreme`, `emissive_background`, `ao_dark`, `dark_metal`) for Task 6. `check_of` maps the codes to their switch: Z sign belongs to Green direction, the scale outlier to Parallax. (superseded by FC3)

- **CL15 The Checks section.** In Advanced, after Interface: "Value range", "Plastic look", "Fake resolution", "Green direction", "Parallax", "UV coverage" and "Drop mip 0 when it adds nothing" (hover: hold textures whose top mip adds almost nothing). The last is disabled until Fake resolution is on ("Turn on Fake resolution first", preflight K22). The boxes' AccessKit names end in "check" ("Value range check", …, "UV coverage check") so they differ from same-text controls (ui-rules 8.4). A section draws from `&AppModel`, so a change pushes `Action::Checks(new)` and `body`'s action loop calls `set_checks`; it works in the drawer and the set panel's Advanced tab (CC3). The settings panel's own list of a texture's check results moved to Task 3 (preflight K5). (amended by FC2, FC9)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC3, 2026-10-29)
- **CL18 Check warnings at export (Task 3).** C1 under layout v2: Problems rows plus one end-of-export summary line, never Review (CA5) and never `export_blocks` / `export_asks`, so a check never asks anything or changes "ready" (test `export_never_asks_about_check_warnings`). (amended by FC3)
  - **Not copied into the Files rows** (owner answer 3, 2026-10-19): they would show twice in Problems after an export. Selection comes from the Problems row's click-through (`select_item`, `select_set`).
  - **Summary.** `checks::checks_summary` counts the `Level::Check` rows of `problems` (so item, set and engine export-row `CHK-*` codes, K2; Info is never a row): "Checks: 3 warnings on 1 texture and 1 set; see Problems" (K19, real plurals, sets apart). `export_ended` notifies it at Info level in the `Ok` arm, once per export, with `messages.notify` (a log line and a toast, no dialog logic, so an open dialog stays and nothing is lost). Task 5 moves it after the final pass. `t` counts distinct item keys and job keys; an item key and a job key are different strings, so a Warn-mode CHK-LOW-DETAIL File row (Task 4) adds its own entry.
  - **Texture list and green line.** The settings panel's warning list now includes the item's check results, Info included (K5, X3). `CHK-GREEN-GUESS` appends " Looks OpenGL (curl test)." to the `IN-GREEN-UNSTATED` line (`_normal`/`_nrm`/`_norm`) and otherwise draws its own small line; both carry the same "Treat as OpenGL (flip green)" button (`Action::Decide(Answer::Green(Opengl))`). AW5 is unchanged: no auto-flip.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC2, 2026-10-29)
- **CL20 Fake-resolution check (check 3, Task 4).** Measured during convert, on the chain export builds, right after coverage preservation (`pipeline::prepare_then`), behind `BatchRequest::low_detail` (`LowDetailMode::{Off, Warn, Hold}`, default `Off`; the CLI never sets it). (amended by FC2)
  - **Metric** (detail energy from 03 / 06): `checks::detail_ratio` = E01 / E12, where E01 is the mean of `(mip0 − nearest_up(mip1))²` over finite RGB samples and E12 the same between mip 1 and mip 2. `None` under 3 levels or when E12 < 1e-6 (a flat image). A native texture scores near or above 1 (white noise about 4), a nearest 2x upscale near 0, a bilinear 2x upscale of white noise about 0.19 and of box-softened noise about 0.24.
  - **Threshold** `LOW_DETAIL_RATIO = 0.25` (the brief's starting value 0.15 was raised). On the local test corpus (`tests/low_detail_corpus.rs`, release): 10,000 jobs, 6,126 profile-eligible, 5,962 measured. Below 0.15: 20; below 0.2: 24; below 0.25: **45** (0.75 %); below 0.3: 211. The histogram is sparse up to 0.25 and climbs steeply after it (166 in [0.25, 0.30), 603 in [0.30, 0.40)). 18 flagged files were spot-checked at 1:1 against mip 1 shown at 2x nearest (`icefloes`, `dwemetaltiles01_n`, `dawnguardhammer2_n`, `smemptywood01`, `silverware`, `farmcloth02shat512_f`, `goldtrim01silver`, `smim_bonemeal`, `horkerwhiskers`, `FXCork`, `hood_f`, `falmervfrozen_n`, `wallbaskets01details02`, `smetaldet01alt`, `wolfpelt_n`, `tropicalshrub01_n`, `sovngardeclutter01_n`, `studdedarmorfem01_body_f`). Every one was a blocky or soft upscale, or nearly flat: mip 1 at 2x looked the same as mip 0. 0.15 would miss the bilinear case and the soft upscales in [0.15, 0.25). Textures just above 0.25 are often soft too; the threshold stops at the knee to keep the warning rare.
  - **Eligibility** (`checks::low_detail_eligible`): `resize = none`, `drop_top_mip` off, a Color or Normal category (never `complex_grass`), at least 3 mip levels, and a size `resize = half` would change (the larger side above `HALF_FLOOR`, T4). A 512² or 512x256 texture is never flagged; 1024x256, 512x1024 and NPOT 1000x600 are measured. `low_detail_profile_eligible` is the half known before Load (everything but the size).
  - **Warn:** the `CHK-LOW-DETAIL` warning (data `{width, height, ratio}`, message "mip 0 adds little detail over mip 1 (r); a WxH texture carries about W/2xH/2 of detail") is added to the written file's report after its manifest record is built. The bytes, the record and its validation summary are those of `Off` (test `warn_and_off_write_identical_bytes_and_off_is_the_default`).
  - **Hold:** the warning is emitted, then `FileSkipped { kind: low_detail, code: CHK-LOW-DETAIL, reason: "held (low detail)" }`, plus "; previous output kept" when the output exists (08d-7). Nothing is written. A held file counts in `skipped` and `skipped_intentional`, so the exit code is unchanged (`BatchSummary::exit_code` doc).
  - **Flagged again next time** (owner ruling, 2026-10-17, Pending answer 2 accepted as written): under `Hold` and `if-changed`, an up-to-date output whose profile passes the profile half of eligibility is not skipped before Load. It runs measure-only (`pipeline::Keep::Measure`, preflight K15: no quantise, encode, hash or write) and comes back `Processed::Held` or `Processed::UpToDate` (a texture that fails the size test after Load, or measures above the threshold, is reported up to date as before). Measure-only skips the source stamp check: `run` has already matched the content by hash, so a touched-but-identical source is measured, not failed (review M10, test `hold_measures_a_touched_but_identical_source`). Cost: one load, preprocess and mip build per eligible up-to-date texture, estimated in research 06 at about +3.6 s per 10k (not measured here), only while Hold is on.
  - **Warn never re-measures an up-to-date output:** it warns only about the files it encodes (review M7); re-flagging is Hold's alone.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FC2, 2026-10-29)
- **CL24 Drop mip 0: hold and choose (Task 5).** With "Fake resolution" and "Drop mip 0 when it adds nothing" on, the GUI exports in `LowDetailMode::Hold` (only "Fake resolution": `Warn`; off: `Off`). The switch never writes the per-file field; it only holds. At the end of the export the held rows (`ExportModel::held`, the size from their `CHK-LOW-DETAIL` data) are offered in the "Export at half size?" box, ticked. The remembered setting is the per-file `drop_top_mip` (CL21), so a ticked texture is then written from mip 1. (superseded by FC2)
  - "Export selected at half size" writes `drop_top_mip = true` for every ticked texture in one undo step ("Half size"), saves, and runs the second pass on the re-plan. "Export all at full size" runs it with no edit. A second pass is always `Warn`, so it never holds again.
  - The box is a `dialogs::dialog` modal (640 px) with no Enter default (both buttons write, so both are `Role::Plain`) and no click-outside close (a stray click would start an export). Esc = "Export all at full size", the spec's "close = full size" (owner answer 4, 2026-10-19). The rows scroll in a thin-bar `ScrollArea` capped at 60 % of the window height.
  - `AppModel::sdc_toml_writable()` (no project, a load error, or the read-only flag) is stat'd once when the box opens and kept in `half_size_unwritable`; the box then shows the reason and offers only full size. If the save still fails after "Export selected", the step is undone and the pass runs at full size with its save skipped (`Continue::HalfSize { save: false }`): never half size without a saved field.
  - No box after a cancel or a whole-export error: the held rows stay "skipped: held (low detail)", and the status line and Messages say "N textures held for half size were not exported". A partly failed export (`failed > 0`, nothing cancelled) still offers the box.
  - When it cannot ask, the held textures are exported at full size with a note: another dialog is open ("… exported at full size (another dialog was open)"; the dialog stays), or a "Stop the export and close / open" answer came too late to cancel anything ("… before closing" / "… before opening the folder"). A close or open asked while the box is open or its pass waits (K12) is kept in `after_export`, and an open box is answered as full size. The close or open runs after that pass.
  - `Continue::HalfSize { save }` re-queues behind a plan in flight (K13) and is exempt from the 08f-1 drop in `finish_plan` and in the scan-read tail (it asks nothing). An Export click while it waits is not queued: it notes "Export waits for the half-size pass" (fix round 1, M1). Before it writes, the pass runs `export_stopped()` and then compares Review's asks with those of the first pass (`export_asked`, the texts when the fresh pass started). A new ask opens Review, so nothing is written past an unticked warning (ui-rules 7.3). Either stop notes "N textures held for half size were not exported: {why}" and runs the finishers.
  - `start_export_pass(fresh, save)` replaces `start_export`. A `fresh = false` pass keeps `pending_json`/`pending_cs`/`pending_cm`, Review's JSON answer, the rows, `drawer_epoch` (the CF12 bar glides on) and the Files tab; it restarts only `export.pace`.
  - The JSON/CS/CM finishers, the checks summary and `run_after_export` run once, after the final pass (08f-6 holds: the error dialog never replaces an open one). Two passes log two "Export finished" lines; the second is prefixed "Held textures:", and `export.summary` holds the second pass (K14).
  - An unticked texture is flagged again next time (spec), including when its output is up to date (the engine's measure-only run, CL20).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC2, 2026-10-29)
- **CL26 The half size in Advanced and in Copy settings (owner rulings O1 and 2026-10-20).** (superseded by FC2)
  - **Advanced.** `drop_top_mip` is an `ADVANCED_FIELDS` row, "Half size (drop mip 0)": a tick box with ↺, like `resize`. ↺ removes the key in one undo step, and unticking also removes it (`set_field` routes `false` to `clear_field`), because a written `false` would stay as a provenance entry (Task 4 M9). On a single-level chain (`mips.levels = "none"` or a count of 0 or 1), `field_lock` disables the row with "No mips to drop", and `set_field` refuses `true` there, so bulk edits and Copy settings never write it either (fix round 1, M6). A half size ticked here, or copied, is never measured first.
  - **Copy settings.** A saved `drop_top_mip` on the source is not a tick in the Copy settings list. Apply (or opening Copy settings when the half size is the source's only field) asks "Copy the half size too?" in a `dialogs::dialog`. This makes the question the single decision point.
    - The buttons are Cancel, "Also copy half size" (Plain) and "Copy without half size". "Copy without half size" is the Enter default, so it is `Role::Default`: the framework's only Enter role. It is the safe side, because the targets' mip 0 was never measured.
    - Esc or a click outside cancels the whole copy.
    - Either answer is one undo step, "Copy settings from …".

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FC2, 2026-10-29)
- **CL27 Exits of a held export, and the pass signature (Task 5, fix round 1).**
  - **A dropped pass still finishes the export (I1).** A failed re-plan (`plan_failed`) or a Reload while the held pass waits calls `drop_half_size(why)`: the note "N textures held for half size were not exported: planning failed: … / the documents were reloaded", then `finish_export`. The first pass's PGPatcher, CS PBR and complex-material JSON is written, and a close or open that K12 deferred happens at once, so it never goes stale. Both helpers run after the failure's or reload's own handling, so a deferred Open does not run in the middle of it. `cancel_open` cannot reach a queued pass: `opening` is `None` during an edit's re-plan.
  - **Stopping the held pass (M2, owner answer 4).** While the held full-size pass runs (`half_pass`), "Stop the export and close / open the folder" does not cancel it. The answer is stored in `after_export` and runs after the pass, because close means full size, then close. The Export button's own Cancel still stops it. (superseded by FC2)
  - **The half-size rows (M7).** After the held pass writes a texture at half size, its first-pass `CHK-LOW-DETAIL` warning is dropped from the export row (`ExportModel::drop_low_detail` at the `fresh = false` start), so Problems and the checks summary no longer list it. An unticked texture is measured again in `Warn` and keeps its warning.
  - **The box (M4).** Each tick box's accessible name is the full path, and its hover shows the full path even while it is disabled. A disabled "Export selected" says why on hover (`dialogs::dialog_hover`). The list draws only the rows on screen (`show_rows`).
  - **`start_export_pass(fresh, save) -> bool` (Track A builds on it, X2).** It returns true when the export started. `run_continue(HalfSize)` uses that to run the finishers when the pass cannot start.
  - Track A's Also export sets `Warn`, so no box follows it (X-S3, when it lands).
  - **Tests.** `tests/low_detail_export.rs` (24) and `model::tests::{closing_during_the_export_writes_held_at_full_size_then_closes, closing_with_the_half_size_box_open_exports_full_size_then_closes, a_failed_replan_while_the_half_size_pass_waits_finishes_the_export, a_reload_while_the_half_size_pass_waits_finishes_the_export, stop_and_close_during_the_held_pass_waits_for_it, close_or_open_while_the_half_size_pass_waits_happen_after_it}`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC13, 2026-10-29)
- **CL28 Range overlay (Task 6).** (superseded by FC13)
  - **Offered only with a per-texel CHK-RANGE (owner, spec "Value-range overlay").** The preview toolbar's "Range" toggle (after "Tile 3×3"; AccessKit name "Range overlay", ui-rules 8.4) is drawn only when one of the expanded texture's CHK-RANGE warnings names a rule of `rules_for(subtype)` (`AppModel::range_overlay_offered`). An albedo whose only range warning is `dark_metal`, or an `_rmaos` with only `ao_dark`, gets no toggle: those are not per-texel rules, so the overlay would paint nothing. Like the heatmap, `ViewState::overlay` is not saved in `gui.toml` (AG59).
  - **What it paints.** Every texel of the source mip on view that breaks one of the rules its warnings name, judged on the 8-bit codes of the stored values (`RangeRule::bad`), in magenta at alpha 160 (`[255, 0, 255, 160]`, `overlay::RANGE_BAD`). The rest is clear.
  - **Built on a worker, painted by egui (no wgpu slot).** `overlay_for(item, mip, kind)` builds from `model.preview.source` only for the expanded item with its source `Ready` (K23; `mip` is relative to `first_level`). There is one job per key: item, mip, kind, and the source `Arc`, so a reloaded source builds again. A result whose generation is not the current one is dropped. The image becomes an egui texture (`Nearest`, `Repeat`), uploaded again only when its `Arc` changes. It is painted over the picture's screen rect only, uv `[0, 1]²` or `[-1, 2)²` with Tile 3×3 (`ui::preview::picture_rects`; `uv_rect` is (offset, scale), A1, K8), clipped to the pane. It is drawn on the source pane only, and not while a pin is compared.
  - **`v2_jobs_pending()` and the one `app.rs` line (owner answer 8, 2026-10-19; reconciliation X13 amended).** `v2_jobs_pending()` is `overlay_pending()` for now; Task 8 and Tracks A and B add their jobs to it. App::ui's `visible` predicate reads it, added in its own commit. It is not part of `busy()`, so the Advanced fields stay enabled while an overlay builds. `wait_idle` returns only when `!busy() && !v2_jobs_pending()`.
  - **A panicking job (K9).** The job body runs through `worker::or_send`, whose failure message is the job's own `WorkerMsg::Overlay { image: None }`: the pending flag clears and the key is not retried every frame. `spawn_guarded` still shows the internal-error dialog, as it does for the heatmap.
  - **Tests.** `tests/overlay.rs` (6: the painted texels, the toggle only with a per-texel warning, built off the UI thread with a stale result dropped, pending without `busy()`, a panic clears pending, the picture rect); `tests/v2_jobs_poll.rs` (`SdcApp` with app data redirected, K11: `repaint_delay <= 50 ms` while the job runs and more once it landed, A4); `ui_smoke::the_toolbar_pin_holds_with_the_range_toggle` (1100, 1280 and 1600 px; `assert_toolbar_layout` takes the conditional controls after "Tile 3×3", K7); `model::tests::an_idle_model_has_no_v2_jobs_pending`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CL44, 2026-10-20)
- **CL31 UV coverage in the GUI (Task 8).**
  - **On click only (owner).** Nothing reads coverage until the user turns on the preview's "UV coverage" toggle (after "Range"; AccessKit name "UV coverage overlay", ui-rules 8.4) or clicks "Check all UV coverage" in Advanced → Checks. The answers live in `AppModel::uv` (`UvState`): a session cache keyed by the scan path the meshes name the output by (`<mesh_key(game_path, output stem)>.dds`, as "Used by meshes" asks), in memory only (C4: no disk cache).
  - **When it is offered (`uv_offered`, cached state only).** In this order: "Wait for the Meshes scan to finish" (CL32: now "A Meshes scan is running or was stopped: wait for it to finish, or scan again") while `scan_store_unsettled` is set (BY6, re-check C12), and then no read and no probe starts; "Scan your MO2 instance in the Meshes view first" without a scan DB, without its file (the per-frame `scan_stamp`), or until the scan-has-rows probe has answered true for the current stamp (so an empty or old-schema scan counts as no scan); "Only colour textures on meshes have UV coverage" for any sub-type but `Diffuse`, `PbrAlbedo` and `SkinAlbedo`. Landscape diffuses are left out (terrain uses LTEX/TXST records, not NIF shapes). Normals share their set's UVs but are not offered (`shortcut:`). The toggle is drawn for every texture (K7) as one `add_enabled` widget (CE20), disabled with that reason, or with "Hidden while comparing" while a pin is compared (as Range, review M3).
  - **The probe.** `scan_has_rows` runs on a worker whenever `scan_stamp` changes, but only while no Meshes scan is unsettled (A12: a running scan changes the stamp every frame). Its answer is kept with the stamp it read.
  - **The cache is cleared** by `used_by_scan_changed` (a finished scan, another folder) and by any change of `scan_stamp` (an external CLI scan). A clear cancels the reads and the Check all in flight; an answer read before it is dropped by its generation.
  - **Reads and errors (K18, review M4 of Task 7).** One read per scan path at a time. A failed read (DB open or query `Err`, or a panic in the read, K9) is cached as a `Coverage` with that error line and `nifs: 0`, so it is not retried every frame. A cancelled read is never cached: the cancel flag decides, never the message text. The texture size passed to `coverage_for` is the written size (`out_size::estimate`, so `resize` and `drop_top_mip` count), or [0, 0] without an analysis, which leaves the gutter unmeasured.
  - **The overlay.** The read's worker also builds the overlay image from the mask (`overlay::uv_overlay_image`, an `Arc<ColorImage>`, CL29), so it is not an overlay job and does not depend on the mip on view. Texels no mesh uses are `[0, 0, 0, 180]`, used ones `[255, 255, 255, 40]`. It is painted like the range overlay: over the picture's rect only, `uv_rect` as (offset, scale), Nearest and Repeat (A1, K8). The toggle's hover gives the used share, the mesh count, the gutter share or "gutter not measured at 4096 px or wider on either axis, or until the size is known" (CL32), and the first five error lines. (Superseded by CL44: the image is built by an overlay job for the expanded item only.)
  - **CHK-UV-GUTTER** (`Warning`) is a `Level::Check` Problems row like the other checks (owner answer 1): "UV islands sit closer than 8 texels for {p} % of used texels: colours bleed across them at distance (mip 3+)", raised by `refresh_checks` from the cache when `gutter > GUTTER_SHARE` and the "UV coverage" switch is on. An unmeasured gutter (`None`) or a failed read raises nothing. It never changes "ready", and the Checks filter and the end summary count it as they count every `Level::Check` row. The cache revision (bumped by every landed answer and every clear) is part of `refresh_checks`' key (K1), so the row follows the next `poll`. `GUTTER_SHARE` was 0.005 here: tuning it needed a scan with NIFs, which the PNG test corpus does not have. Superseded by CL34.
  - **Check all** covers every offered item of the open folder (C4) whose scan path is not cached yet, on `settings.jobs` scoped threads (0 = every core) inside one `spawn_guarded` job. It sends one `UvCoverage` and one `UvProgress` per texture and a final `UvProgress { finished: true }`; `finished` ends the run, also when cancelled with `done < total`. With nothing to read it sends `(0, 0)` once. While it runs, Advanced shows a bar of "{done} / {total}" drawn with `.animate(false)`, whose fill glides only through `ui::header::shown_progress` with the epoch `1 << 63 | run` (K10: never the export bar's `drawer_epoch`; reduce motion through `move_dur`, CF1, CF12), and "Stop checking UV coverage" (ui-rules 5.4). The button is disabled with the scan reason, or the colour-texture reason when the folder has no colour texture (`UvState::any_colour`, set in `refresh_checks`, so the per-frame test is O(1), ui-rules 9.5). A panic outside the reads still ends the run (`or_send`, K9).
  - **Polling (owner answer 8).** `v2_jobs_pending()` = `overlay_pending() || uv.read_pending() || uv.all_running() || uv.probe_pending()`, read by the one `app.rs` line. Nothing is added to `busy()`, so Check all never greys the Advanced fields. The UI thread never opens the scan DB.
  - **Messages.** `WorkerMsg::UvCoverage` carries `generation` and the ready `image` beside `tex` and `coverage`; `UvProgress` carries `run` and `finished`; `ScanHasRows` carries the `stamp` it read. These fields drop stale answers.
  - **Tests.** `tests/uv_coverage.rs` adds 12: disabled without a scan, with an empty scan and for a landscape diffuse; not offered while unsettled, with no probe mid-scan; Check all polls without greying Advanced; Check all disabled with the reason; the cache and its clear on a finished scan; a failed read cached and a cancelled one not (K18); a panicking read clears pending (K9); CHK-UV-GUTTER after a read, its switch and the share edge; the scoped runner with the cancel flag set, with 0 items, and a full run (K17); the UI thread never reads the scan DB, and the read landed; the overlay colours. `ui_smoke::the_toolbar_pin_holds_with_the_uv_toggle` pins the toggle, enabled and disabled, at 1100, 1280 and 1600 px; `TOOLBAR_ORDER` has "UV coverage overlay" after "Tile 3×3" (K7).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC13 and Note CL34, 2026-10-29)
- **CL32 UV coverage, fix round 1 (Task 8 review of 1c6b8ee).**
  - **The written size is part of the cache key (I1).** Keys are (scan path, written `[w, h]`). Nothing is read until the analysis gives the size (`uv_key` is `None` then), so a `[0, 0]` answer is never cached and the read starts once the analysis lands. A new written size (a `resize` edit, `drop_top_mip`) reads again. Check all skips items whose size is not known yet. `WorkerMsg::UvCoverage` carries the `size`.
  - **A failed read paints nothing (I2).** A cached answer with no NIF read and an error line (`uv_coverage::failed`) has no overlay image. The toggle is disabled with its error lines as the hover, and the answer stays cached (K18). A true "no mesh uses it" answer (no error) keeps its all-dim overlay.
  - **Hover wording (M1, M2).** "gutter not measured at 4096 px or wider on either axis, or until the size is known", and "{p} % of used texels sit closer than 8 texels to another UV island", the warning's wording.
  - **Check all (M3, M4).** The bar's AccessKit name is "Checking UV coverage: {done} of {total}". After Stop, a disabled "Stopping…" stands in for the button until the reads in flight finish. The button is disabled with "Turn on UV coverage check first" while the switch is off.
  - **Stamp change (M5).** A new scan stamp clears and cancels a running Check all even when the cache is empty.
  - **A stopped scan (M6, deviation from the brief's text).** The brief's "Wait for the Meshes scan to finish" is replaced by "A Meshes scan is running or was stopped: wait for it to finish, or scan again". The model sees only `scan_store_unsettled`, which the app sets for a running scan and leaves set after a cancelled or failed one (app.rs). Telling the two apart needs a second app flag, an `app.rs` edit that owner answer 8 does not allow, so one text covers both. (superseded by FC13)
  - **Another folder keeps the cache (M7).** Its key does not depend on the folder. `used_by_scan_changed` (also called by `open_folder`) now only drops the answers in flight (`UvState::drop_in_flight`: a new generation, and the reads and Check all cancelled). A finished scan clears the cache in `refresh_mesh_votes(false)`, and a new stamp still clears it.
  - **Batched check refresh (M8).** A single read (or an injected answer with no run) bumps the cache revision at once. Answers of a running Check all bump it at most every 250 ms (`REFRESH_EVERY`, from `uv_poll`), and once when the run ends, so `refresh_checks` does not run on every poll of a long run.
  - **v orientation, checked.** Mask row 0 is v = 0, and it is the overlay image's top row. DirectX UVs and the DDS on screen have v = 0 at the top, and `picture_rects` maps uv (0, 0) to the picture's top-left. `the_overlay_puts_uv_v0_at_the_top` pins it with an L-shaped island.
  - **Tests.** `tests/uv_coverage.rs` adds 7:
    - `an_unknown_size_reads_nothing_and_a_size_change_reads_again`;
    - `a_failed_read_paints_no_overlay_and_disables_the_toggle`;
    - `check_all_is_disabled_while_the_uv_switch_is_off`;
    - `a_stamp_change_cancels_check_all_on_an_empty_cache`;
    - `opening_another_folder_keeps_the_uv_cache`;
    - `check_all_answers_refresh_the_checks_in_batches`;
    - `the_overlay_puts_uv_v0_at_the_top`.
    
    `check_all_polls_without_greying_advanced` now also checks the bar's name and "Stopping…".

(Superseded by CL42: it passes at 1.12.)

Run 2: base 34.7 s (34.7, 34.7, 35.2), checks 44.8 s (44.5, 44.8, 44.9), ratio 1.290. The base matches research 06 (34.9 to 36.7 s). The checks cost is stable at about +10 s, +29 %, over the +15 % budget (about +5.2 s).

Options: profile and optimise; run the curl and steep passes only when the Green or Parallax switch is on (today `hints_for` gates them by suffix and `cache.checks` only); or an owner ruling to raise the budget. Until one of these lands, the ignored test stays red when run.

The last is disabled until Fake resolution is on ("Turn on Fake resolution first", preflight K22). ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL20 Fake-resolution check (check 3, Task 4).** Measured during convert, on the chain export builds, right after coverage preservation (`pipeline::prepare_then`), behind `BatchRequest::low_detail` (`LowDetailMode::{Off, Warn, Hold}`, default `Off`; the CLI never sets it).
  - **Metric** (detail energy from 03 / 06): `checks::detail_ratio` = E01 / E12, where E01 is the mean of `(mip0 − nearest_up(mip1))²` over finite RGB samples and E12 the same between mip 1 and mip 2. `None` under 3 levels or when E12 < 1e-6 (a flat image). A native texture scores near or above 1 (white noise about 4), a nearest 2x upscale near 0, a bilinear 2x upscale of white noise about 0.19 and of box-softened noise about 0.24.
  - **Threshold** `LOW_DETAIL_RATIO = 0.25` (the brief's starting value 0.15 was raised). On the local test corpus (`tests/low_detail_corpus.rs`, release): 10,000 jobs, 6,126 profile-eligible, 5,962 measured. Below 0.15: 20; below 0.2: 24; below 0.25: **45** (0.75 %); below 0.3: 211. The histogram is sparse up to 0.25 and climbs steeply after it (166 in [0.25, 0.30), 603 in [0.30, 0.40)). 18 flagged files were spot-checked at 1:1 against mip 1 shown at 2x nearest (`icefloes`, `dwemetaltiles01_n`, `dawnguardhammer2_n`, `smemptywood01`, `silverware`, `farmcloth02shat512_f`, `goldtrim01silver`, `smim_bonemeal`, `horkerwhiskers`, `FXCork`, `hood_f`, `falmervfrozen_n`, `wallbaskets01details02`, `smetaldet01alt`, `wolfpelt_n`, `tropicalshrub01_n`, `sovngardeclutter01_n`, `studdedarmorfem01_body_f`). Every one was a blocky or soft upscale, or nearly flat: mip 1 at 2x looked the same as mip 0. 0.15 would miss the bilinear case and the soft upscales in [0.15, 0.25). Textures just above 0.25 are often soft too; the threshold stops at the knee to keep the warning rare.
  - **Eligibility** (`checks::low_detail_eligible`): `resize = none`, `drop_top_mip` off, a Color or Normal category (never `complex_grass`), at least 3 mip levels, and a size `resize = half` would change (the larger side above `HALF_FLOOR`, T4). A 512² or 512x256 texture is never flagged; 1024x256, 512x1024 and NPOT 1000x600 are measured. `low_detail_profile_eligible` is the half known before Load (everything but the size).
  - **Warn:** the `CHK-LOW-DETAIL` warning (data `{width, height, ratio}`, message "mip 0 adds little detail over mip 1 (r); a WxH texture carries about W/2xH/2 of detail") is added to the written file's report after its manifest record is built. The bytes, the record and its validation summary are those of `Off` (test `warn_and_off_write_identical_bytes_and_off_is_the_default`).
  - **Hold:** measures and records `CHK-LOW-DETAIL` but writes the full chain, byte for byte what an Off run writes; the GUI asks about half size in Review (Note FC2).
  - **Flagged again next time** (owner ruling, 2026-10-17, Pending answer 2 accepted as written): under `Hold` and `if-changed`, an up-to-date output whose profile passes the profile half of eligibility is not skipped before Load. It runs measure-only (`pipeline::Keep::Measure`, preflight K15: no quantise, encode, hash or write) and comes back `Processed::UpToDate`, with the warning when it measures low (Note FC2), (a texture that fails the size test after Load, or measures above the threshold, is reported up to date as before). Measure-only skips the source stamp check: `run` has already matched the content by hash, so a touched-but-identical source is measured, not failed (review M10, test `hold_measures_a_touched_but_identical_source`). Cost: one load, preprocess and mip build per eligible up-to-date texture, estimated in research 06 at about +3.6 s per 10k (not measured here), only while Hold is on.
  - **Warn never re-measures an up-to-date output:** it warns only about the files it encodes (review M7); re-flagging is Hold's alone.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL26 The half size in Advanced and in Copy settings (owner rulings O1 and 2026-10-20).**
  - **Advanced.** `drop_top_mip` is an `ADVANCED_FIELDS` row, "Half size" (Note FC2): a tick box with ↺, like `resize`. ↺ removes the key in one undo step, and unticking also removes it (`set_field` routes `false` to `clear_field`), because a written `false` would stay as a provenance entry (Task 4 M9). On a single-level chain (`mips.levels = "none"` or a count of 0 or 1), `field_lock` disables the row with "No mips to drop", and `set_field` refuses `true` there, so bulk edits and Copy settings never write it either (fix round 1, M6). A half size ticked here, or copied, is never measured first.
  - **Copy settings.** A saved half size is a tick like any other field (`Half size: true`); there is no dialog (Note FC2).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
  - Carried to Task 5: `out_size` writes n−1 levels for `Count(n)` (M4); Clear removes the key rather than writing `false` (M9); `check_of(ChkLowDetail)`; the export model accepting LowDetail and UpToDate skips on a running row; the "Drop mip 0" switch sets Hold mode, not `Profile::drop_top_mip`.

- **CL25 Export rows and the size estimate (Task 5).** `check_of(CHK-LOW-DETAIL) = LowDetail`, so a Warn-mode export row is a `Level::Check` Problems row: hidden by the "Fake resolution" switch, counted by the Checks filter and the end summary, and never in the Export dot's `ready_rows` (K2). `ExportModel` accepts a `LowDetail` or `UpToDate` skip on a `Running` row (the engine emits `FileStarted` before a held or measure-only run); every other skip keeps the old rule, and neither calls `finished_at`, so the pace ignores them. `out_size::estimate` drops level 0 for `drop_top_mip` on a chain of more than one level: half the size and one level less (`Count(n)` writes n − 1; `full` is a full chain of the half size).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL1 Artist check stats in the analysis (Task 1).** `engine::checks` measures checks 1, 2, 4 and 5 inside the per-image analysis (`analyse_with`, one extra `code8` per texel in the existing loop) and stores them as `Analysis::checks: Option<CheckStats>`.
  - **Bands** per channel (R, G, B, A): `eq0`, `le4`, `low` (codes `<= 25`, about 0.1), `high` (`>= 230`, about 0.9), `ge251`, `eq255`. 25/230 come from research 03; `le4` / `ge251` bound the complex-material parallax window (spec: parallax only strictly between 4/255 and 251/255).
  - **Luma** on the 8-bit codes with Rec. 709 weights, rounded: dark `< 30`, bright `> 240`, black `<= 8` (03 and the spec).
  - **Curl** (check 4, normal green direction): x and y decoded from R and G, z **rebuilt** as `sqrt(max(0, 1 - x² - y²))` and clamped to at least 0.1 (blue is ignored, so BC5's zero blue reads the same; the decoding Track B's `from_normal::slopes` uses, X-M4). Slopes `p = -x/z`, `q = -y/z` in image space, y down; **central differences over interior texels only** (controller ruling, review I1/I2, overriding the brief's "forward differences with wrap"): a map that does not tile gets no seam term, and the central stencil commutes with `bump_normals`' (and Sobel's), so a correct map's as-stored curl is 0 up to rounding even for pixel-scale detail (forward differences left about 0.6 of flipped on white-noise height). `as_stored = mean((∂q/∂x - ∂p/∂y)²)`, `flipped = mean((-∂q/∂x - ∂p/∂y)²)`; non-finite samples are skipped, and the result is 0 when none remain (also under 3x3). A real height field has no curl, so the smaller value names the green convention. One rolling three-row slope buffer, so each texel is decoded once. Track B had not landed, so `checks.rs` has its own `slopes` (`shortcut:` comment; merge when Track B lands).
  - **Steep steps** (check 5): the share of horizontal and vertical interior neighbour pairs whose codes differ by at least `STEEP_CODES = 32` (research 06), in R for `_p` and in A for `_m`; 0 when there are no pairs.
  - **`two_channel`** is `SourceInfo::channels == 2`: BC5 / RG8 DDS inputs (`load.rs:239`), but also a grey+alpha PNG (`load.rs:131`), so such an `_n` skips the Z-sign part of check 4 (preflight T1).
  - **Non-finite samples (review M1).** NaN counts as code 0 in bands, luma and steep (what `code8` gives, as in the existing alpha histogram); `curl` skips non-finite texels. Preprocess replaces them before output (S9).
  - **Reading the bands (review M2).** With no source alpha, A is 1.0, so `bands[3]` counts every texel in `ge251` and `eq255`: rules on alpha bands gate on `SourceInfo::has_alpha`. For `two_channel`, B decodes as 0, so `bands[2].eq0 == texels` and luma is R and G only.
  - **`steep` for `_m` (review M5).** Hints measure A for every `m`, VanillaM too, where A is an env mask rather than a height: consumers ignore `steep` unless the shader is complex material (CPM).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL2 Hints by canonical suffix.** `checks::hints_for(suffix, cache.checks)`: bands and luma for every texture; curl for canonical `n` (every vocabulary normal, `normal`, `nrm`, `norm`, `normalgl`, `normaldx`, canonicalises to `n`); steps for `p` (R) and `m` (A, also VanillaM: a harmless extra pass). Pass 1 takes the suffix from the file's split (now computed once, before the cache lookup); pass 2 from `RecipeKind::suffix()`. The file cache requires matching hints (Note DZ), so a retype that changes the suffix re-measures; a `[files]` `subtype` on a file with no suffix maps to a suffix through `checks::hints_for_subtype` (Note FC13).

- **CL5 Artist check warnings (GUI, Task 2).** Placement under layout v2 (Q1, owner answers 1-2, 2026-10-19; Checks filter, owner 2026-10-20; checks are not amber, Note FC3).
  - `model::checks::texture_checks` turns one texture's cached stats (`TextureItem::analysis`, filled in `PlanView::build` from `AnalysisCache::analysis`; `None` for skipped, failed and packed-source items) into `CHK-*` warnings; `refresh_checks` adds dark metal per set and the landscape scale outliers. The results live only in `AppModel`'s check maps (`check_warnings(item)`, `set_check_warnings(set)`), never in `TextureItem::warnings` or `SetView::set_warnings`, so `tile_ready` and the card's "⚠ n" never see them.
  - Warning-level results are Problems rows at `bottom::Level::Check` (after `Warning`). `counts_as_ready_problem` is false for them, and `flagged` inserts only rows that pass it, so the set and card dots, the Sets filter's "Not ready" and the header Export dot ignore checks; the tile dots read `tile_ready` (`item.warnings`), which never holds a check result. `Flagged` also counts `ready_rows` (the Export dot and its "M problems" hover) and `check_rows` (the Problems tab's "Checks {n}"), so neither scans the list per frame (preflight K6). Review's "N other warnings" still counts `Level::Warning` only.

  - Each Check row's dot is the weak colour (`Status::Check`, labelled "check"), not amber (Note FC3).

Plastic is a Warning with soft text ("may look plastic"; spec: soft by design, mesh spec values vary); the green guess is Info because the curl test is weak on straight ridges and never auto-flips (spec C3, Note AW). - **CL8 Thresholds, tuned on the 10k set (Step 6, review I2; `pub const` in `model/checks.rs`).** `tests/checks_corpus.rs` (`#[ignore]`, `SDC_TEXTURES_10K`, read only) plans the local test corpus (`SDC_TEXTURES_10K`; cs, recursive): 10,000 jobs, among them 2,421 `pbr_albedo`, 2,528 `rmaos`, 1,338 `_p` (Height), 2,517 normal-category (52 vanilla `_n`), 131 emissive (123 `pbr_emissive`, 8 `glow`). Each threshold, its source, and how many it flags at the final value (the starting value's count in brackets):
  - `RANGE_SHARE 0.50` (was 0.05; albedo luma < 30 or > 240, research 03 / PBR guidance): 209 of 2,421 albedos, 8.6 % (1,033, 43 %). The share is mostly dark texels (dark-share p50 0.02, p75 0.15, p90 0.42; bright p99 0.19); alpha-cut albedos run higher (out-of-range p50 0.12 against 0.01 opaque), because their transparent texels are often black. shortcut: the stats count every texel; alpha-weighted bands would let the threshold come back down for cutout albedos.
  - `METAL_GREY_SHARE 0.40` (was 0.20; G 26..=229): 230 of 2,528 `_rmaos`, 9.1 % (488, 19 %; grey share p75 0.13, p90 0.38).
  - `ROUGH_EXTREME_SHARE 0.50` (R 0 or 255): 9 (kept). `AO_DARK_MEAN 0.35` (B mean): 1 (kept).
  - `EMISSIVE_BLACK_MIN 0.25` (was 0.50; luma code <= 8): 18 of 131, 14 % (27, 21 %; black share p25 0.60, p50 0.84).
  - `PLASTIC_SHARE 0.95` (alpha >= 250, or no alpha): 6 of 52 vanilla `_n`, kept. "No alpha" is the intended warning: the spec's check table lists plastic look as "analysis already counts alpha >= 250 / alpha missing", and a vanilla `_n` without alpha has a flat, full specular. It stays a soft Warning ("may look plastic") with its own switch. The repo fixture's flat `rock01_normal.png` raises it for that reason (K3).
  - `GREEN_RATIO 0.5` with `CURL_FLOOR 1e-4`: 34 of 2,517 normals, 1.35 % (under the brief's ~2 % target; flipped/as-stored curl p1 0.45, p5 1.01, p50 2.8). Kept. Z sign: 1.
  - Parallax on 1,338 `_p`: range use under `RANGE_USE_CODES 16` codes (was 32): 102, 7.6 % (175, 13 %; range-used p5 11, p25 128); clipping `CLIP_SHARE 0.10`: 69, 5.2 % (kept; clip share p95 0.22); white-heavy (mean > 0.75): 41, 3.1 % (kept); steep `STEEP_SHARE 0.03` (was 0.01): 30, 2.2 % (96, 7.2 %; steep share p90 0.007, p95 0.015, p99 0.049).
  - `DARK_METAL_SHARE 0.50` and `SCALE_FACTOR 2.0` are set-level and not in the corpus run (it plans files, not GUI sets); kept at their starting values.
  - A share over zero texels is 0.0 (no warning).
