ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL28 Range overlay (Task 6).**
  - **Enabled only with a per-texel CHK-RANGE (owner, spec "Value-range overlay").** The preview toolbar's "Range" toggle (after "Tile 3×3"; AccessKit name "Range overlay", ui-rules 8.4) is drawn for every texture and enabled only when one of the expanded texture's CHK-RANGE warnings names a rule of `rules_for(subtype)` (`AppModel::range_overlay_offered`); otherwise it is disabled with "This texture has no value-range warning to paint" (Note FC13). An albedo whose only range warning is `dark_metal`, or an `_rmaos` with only `ao_dark`, gets a disabled toggle: those are not per-texel rules, so the overlay would paint nothing. Like the heatmap, `ViewState::overlay` is not saved in `gui.toml` (AG59).
  - **What it paints.** Every texel of the source mip on view that breaks one of the rules its warnings name, judged on the 8-bit codes of the stored values (`RangeRule::bad`), in magenta at alpha 160 (`[255, 0, 255, 160]`, `overlay::RANGE_BAD`). The rest is clear.
  - **Built on a worker, painted by egui (no wgpu slot).** `overlay_for(item, mip, kind)` builds from `model.preview.source` only for the expanded item with its source `Ready` (K23; `mip` is relative to `first_level`). There is one job per key: item, mip, kind, and the source `Arc`, so a reloaded source builds again. A result whose generation is not the current one is dropped. The image becomes an egui texture (`Nearest`, `Repeat`), uploaded again only when its `Arc` changes. It is painted over the picture's screen rect only, uv `[0, 1]²` or `[-1, 2)²` with Tile 3×3 (`ui::preview::picture_rects`; `uv_rect` is (offset, scale), A1, K8), clipped to the pane. It is drawn on the source pane only, and not while a pin is compared.
  - **`v2_jobs_pending()` and the one `app.rs` line (owner answer 8, 2026-10-19; reconciliation X13 amended).** `v2_jobs_pending()` is `overlay_pending()` for now; Task 8 and Tracks A and B add their jobs to it. App::ui's `visible` predicate reads it, added in its own commit. It is not part of `busy()`, so the Advanced fields stay enabled while an overlay builds. `wait_idle` returns only when `!busy() && !v2_jobs_pending()`.
  - **A panicking job (K9).** The job body runs through `worker::or_send`, whose failure message is the job's own `WorkerMsg::Overlay { image: None }`: the pending flag clears and the key is not retried every frame. `spawn_guarded` still shows the internal-error dialog, as it does for the heatmap.
  - **Tests.** `tests/overlay.rs` (6: the painted texels, the toggle only with a per-texel warning, built off the UI thread with a stale result dropped, pending without `busy()`, a panic clears pending, the picture rect); `tests/v2_jobs_poll.rs` (`SdcApp` with app data redirected, K11: `repaint_delay <= 50 ms` while the job runs and more once it landed, A4); `ui_smoke::the_toolbar_pin_holds_with_the_range_toggle` (1100, 1280 and 1600 px; `assert_toolbar_layout` takes the conditional controls after "Tile 3×3", K7); `model::tests::an_idle_model_has_no_v2_jobs_pending`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL29 Range overlay, fix round 1 (Task 6 review of bfd5e14).**
  - **The worker hands over a ready `ColorImage` (I1).** The job converts its RGBA to `egui::ColorImage` itself. `OverlayState::image`, `overlay_for` and `WorkerMsg::Overlay` carry `Arc<ColorImage>` instead of `HeatImage`; `range_overlay` and the test builders still return `HeatImage`. The UI uploads that `Arc` as `ImageData::Color` (`load_texture` once, then `TextureHandle::set`), so the UI thread does no per-texel work. Task 8's UV overlay uses the same type.
  - **No blink on a rebuild (M1).** A rebuild for another mip or rule set of the same item and kind keeps the overlay on show until the new one lands. Another item's or kind's overlay is dropped at once, never shown over this one.
  - **The rule set is part of the reuse key (M2).** A new per-texel warning on the same source builds again.
  - **Compared pin (M3).** The toggle is disabled with the hover "Hidden while comparing", because the compared pin replaces the source pane the overlay is drawn on.
  - **Nearest minification (M4),** so one bad texel stays visible when zoomed out.
  - **Freed when not shown (M7).** Turning the toggle off (or the texture losing its offer) drops the model's image and the texture; `collapse` clears `OverlayState`. A job still running then lands and only clears `running`.
  - **Deferred:** M5 (the lens shows no overlay), M6 (the toggle carries across items, like Tile 3×3), M8 (docs, Task 10).
  - **Tests.** `tests/overlay.rs` adds `the_ui_uploads_the_workers_image_and_frees_it_when_off`, `in_split_the_overlay_is_laid_out_on_the_viewport_and_clipped_to_the_source_side` (T1) and `the_range_toggle_is_disabled_while_comparing_a_pin`. `model::overlay::tests` adds `a_new_range_warning_on_the_same_source_rebuilds_the_overlay` and `a_mip_change_keeps_the_overlay_until_the_new_one_lands_and_collapse_frees_it`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL31 UV coverage in the GUI (Task 8).**
  - **On click only (owner).** Nothing reads coverage until the user turns on the preview's "UV coverage" toggle (after "Range"; AccessKit name "UV coverage overlay", ui-rules 8.4) or clicks "Check all UV coverage" in Advanced → Checks. The answers live in `AppModel::uv` (`UvState`): a session cache keyed by the scan path the meshes name the output by (`<mesh_key(game_path, output stem)>.dds`, as "Used by meshes" asks), in memory only (C4: no disk cache).
  - **When it is offered (`uv_offered`, cached state only).** In this order: "Wait for the Meshes scan to finish" while a scan runs, or "The Meshes scan was stopped: scan again to check UV coverage" after a stopped one (Note FC13), while `scan_store_unsettled` is set (BY6, re-check C12), and then no read and no probe starts; "Scan your MO2 instance in the Meshes view first" without a scan DB, without its file (the per-frame `scan_stamp`), or until the scan-has-rows probe has answered true for the current stamp (so an empty or old-schema scan counts as no scan); "Only colour textures on meshes have UV coverage" for any sub-type but `Diffuse`, `PbrAlbedo` and `SkinAlbedo`. Landscape diffuses are left out (terrain uses LTEX/TXST records, not NIF shapes). Normals share their set's UVs but are not offered (`shortcut:`). The toggle is drawn for every texture (K7) as one `add_enabled` widget (CE20), disabled with that reason, or with "Hidden while comparing" while a pin is compared (as Range, review M3).
  - **The probe.** `scan_has_rows` runs on a worker whenever `scan_stamp` changes, but only while no Meshes scan is unsettled (A12: a running scan changes the stamp every frame). Its answer is kept with the stamp it read.
  - **The cache is cleared** by `used_by_scan_changed` (a finished scan, another folder) and by any change of `scan_stamp` (an external CLI scan). A clear cancels the reads and the Check all in flight; an answer read before it is dropped by its generation.
  - **Reads and errors (K18, review M4 of Task 7).** One read per scan path at a time. A failed read (DB open or query `Err`, or a panic in the read, K9) is cached as a `Coverage` with that error line and `nifs: 0`, so it is not retried every frame. A cancelled read is never cached: the cancel flag decides, never the message text. The texture size passed to `coverage_for` is the written size (`out_size::estimate`, so `resize` and `drop_top_mip` count), or [0, 0] without an analysis, which leaves the gutter unmeasured.
  - **The overlay.** The cache keeps masks only; the overlay image (`overlay::uv_overlay_image`, CL29) is built for the expanded item (Note CL44). Texels no mesh uses are `[0, 0, 0, 180]`, used ones `[255, 255, 255, 40]`. It is painted like the range overlay: over the picture's rect only, `uv_rect` as (offset, scale), Nearest and Repeat (A1, K8). The toggle's hover gives the used share, the mesh count, the gutter share or "gutter not measured at 4096 px or wider on either axis, or until the size is known" (CL32), and the first five error lines.
  - **CHK-UV-GUTTER** (`Warning`) is a `Level::Check` Problems row like the other checks (owner answer 1): "UV islands sit closer than 8 texels for {p} % of used texels: colours bleed across them at distance (mip 3+)", raised by `refresh_checks` from the cache when `gutter > GUTTER_SHARE` and the "UV coverage" switch is on. An unmeasured gutter (`None`) or a failed read raises nothing. It never changes "ready", and the Checks filter and the end summary count it as they count every `Level::Check` row. The cache revision (bumped by every landed answer and every clear) is part of `refresh_checks`' key (K1), so the row follows the next `poll`. `GUTTER_SHARE` is 0.02 (Note CL34).
  - **Check all** covers every offered item of the open folder (C4) whose scan path is not cached yet, on one scoped thread per core inside one `spawn_guarded` job. It sends one `UvCoverage` and one `UvProgress` per texture and a final `UvProgress { finished: true }`; `finished` ends the run, also when cancelled with `done < total`. With nothing to read it sends `(0, 0)` once. While it runs, Advanced shows a bar of "{done} / {total}" drawn with `.animate(false)`, and "Stop checking UV coverage" (ui-rules 5.4). The button is disabled with the scan reason, or the colour-texture reason when the folder has no colour texture (`UvState::any_colour`, set in `refresh_checks`, so the per-frame test is O(1), ui-rules 9.5). A panic outside the reads still ends the run (`or_send`, K9).
  - **Polling (owner answer 8).** `v2_jobs_pending()` = `overlay_pending() || uv.read_pending() || uv.all_running() || uv.probe_pending()`, read by the one `app.rs` line. Nothing is added to `busy()`, so Check all never greys the Advanced fields. The UI thread never opens the scan DB.
  - **Messages.** `WorkerMsg::UvCoverage` carries `generation` and `size` beside `tex` and `coverage`, and no image (Note CL44); `UvProgress` carries `run` and `finished`; `ScanHasRows` carries the `stamp` it read. These fields drop stale answers.
  - **Tests.** `tests/uv_coverage.rs` adds 12: disabled without a scan, with an empty scan and for a landscape diffuse; not offered while unsettled, with no probe mid-scan; Check all polls without greying Advanced; Check all disabled with the reason; the cache and its clear on a finished scan; a failed read cached and a cancelled one not (K18); a panicking read clears pending (K9); CHK-UV-GUTTER after a read, its switch and the share edge; the scoped runner with the cancel flag set, with 0 items, and a full run (K17); the UI thread never reads the scan DB, and the read landed; the overlay colours. `ui_smoke::the_toolbar_pin_holds_with_the_uv_toggle` pins the toggle, enabled and disabled, at 1100, 1280 and 1600 px; `TOOLBAR_ORDER` has "UV coverage overlay" after "Tile 3×3" (K7).

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL37 CHK-PLASTIC wording on hair and Advanced Skin (owner, 2026-10-20).** Community Shaders does not read the `_n` alpha for specular on hair or Advanced Skin. CHK-PLASTIC still fires there with the same code, the same Warning level and no data. Its message keeps the CL7 sentence and adds "Only matters for vanilla: Community Shaders doesn't read this normal map's alpha for specular on hair" ("skin" on an Advanced Skin set). `texture_checks` takes the set's shader, which is `refresh_checks`' shader choice or pick. An Advanced Skin `_n` normally resolves `pbr_normal`, because `_rfaos` makes the set PBR, and CHK-PLASTIC only reads a `normal`. The skin wording therefore shows only when the user re-types that `_n` to `normal`.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL38 No CHK-PLASTIC on an `_msn` (owner, 2026-10-20).** The owner ruled that an `_msn` whose set has an `_s` gets no CHK-PLASTIC, because the specular comes from the `_s`. This already held: the check reads only the `normal` sub-type, and an `_msn` resolves `msn`, with or without an `_s`. No code changed. `an_msn_raises_no_plastic_with_or_without_an_s` pins it.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL42 Check stats made cheaper; the budget passes (Task 10 fix round 1, 2026-10-20).** CL41's +29 % was profiled with a scratch test (single thread, 250 corpus files, every 40th, 262 M texels; per texel, release): load 13.7 ns, the analysis without checks 10.7 ns, the band and luma counts **+10.6 ns** on every texture, curl 3.4 ns (on about a quarter of the files, the `n` ones), steep 6.0 ns (on about an eighth, the `p` and `m` ones). The band counts were most of the cost: 24 band counters and 3 luma counters, all `u64`, do not fit in registers, so every texel read, added and wrote about 27 memory counters. Three changes, none of which changes a result:
  - **Bands from histograms.** `analyse_with` counts each texel's R, G, B, A and luma code into five 256-bin histograms (`checks::CodeHist`, five increments per texel). `CheckStats::from_hist` folds them into the same `BandCounts` and luma counts once, after the loop. This replaces the per-texel `CheckStats::add`. The bands now cost +3.3 ns per texel.
  - **`luma_code` without libm.** On baseline x86-64, `f64::round` is a libm call. `luma_code` now rounds by truncating and comparing the exact fraction with 0.5, which equals `round()` for v >= 0. `luma_code_uses_rec_709_weights_rounded` checks all 256³ code triples against `round()`. This change alone did not move the profile; it is kept because it is exact and also serves the GUI's per-texel range overlay.
  - **`steep_share` reads each code once.** Each row's 8-bit codes are taken once and kept for the next row's vertical pairs; before, `code8` ran about three times per texel through `img.at`. Steep went from 6.0 to 2.1 ns per texel. Curl is unchanged.
  - **Same results.** A scratch test compared the new bands, luma counts and steep share with a copy of the old per-texel code on the same 250 corpus files: bit-identical. The `checks` and `analyse` unit tests and `session_cache` pass unchanged, and golden 97/97 is unchanged.
  - **Medians (release, 2026-10-20), two full runs of `check_budget`.** Run 1: base 34.0 s (33.8, 34.0, 34.1), checks 38.0 s (37.8, 38.0, 38.3), ratio 1.119. Run 2: base 34.0 s (33.8, 34.0, 34.1), checks 37.9 s (37.9, 37.9, 38.1), ratio 1.116. The test passes (budget 1.15). The checks now cost about +4 s instead of +10 s.
  - **CLI dry run** (`sdc convert --dry-run --recursive` on the corpus, this branch's release `sdc.exe`, three runs): 34.0, 32.8 and 32.5 s, against 06's 34.9 to 36.7 s. That is unchanged: the CLI computes no stats (CL3). Every run exits 1 because 9,997 files are skipped as low-confidence, with 0 failed.
  - **Peak memory, engine process only.** `tessalith.exe` takes no folder argument, and launching it reads and writes the user data folders, so record-mem.ps1 was not run against the GUI. A scratch test ran one `plan_cached` on the corpus per process (thumbnails on), and the process's peak working set was polled. Checks off: 1138 and 1122 MB. Checks on: 1138 and 1134 MB. The difference is within run-to-run noise; the only new allocation is a 10 KB histogram per analysis in flight. The GUI measurement against the spec estimate (under +40 MB) is still open.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-03)
- **CL44 The UV cache keeps masks only; the overlay image is built for the expanded item (final review I2, 2026-10-20).** This supersedes CL31's "the read's worker also builds the overlay image" (CL31 has a pointer).
  - **Why.** Every read, Check all included, built a 512² `ColorImage` (1 MiB) and the session cache kept it next to the 32 KiB mask. Check all over 2,000 colour textures held about 2 GiB until the scan changed, against the spec's +40 MB.
  - **Now.** The cache is `HashMap<Key, Arc<Coverage>>`; `WorkerMsg::UvCoverage` carries no image. `AppModel::uv_overlay(item)` (now `&mut self`) takes the cached mask and calls `uv_overlay_job`, which builds `overlay::uv_overlay_image` in an overlay job (`spawn_guarded`, the same `OverlayState` and `WorkerMsg::Overlay` as the range overlay), only for the expanded item (`preview.item`). One job per mask (a `Weak<UvMask>` in `OverlayState`, compared by pointer), so a new size or a re-read builds again; the previous image stays while the same item rebuilds. A failed read still paints nothing. At most one overlay image is held at a time, and collapsing or switching the toggle off frees it as before (CL29 M7). The UI thread still does no per-texel work (CL29).
  - **`ui/preview.rs`.** The Uv branch no longer clears `model.overlay`: the UV image now lives there, and `OverlayState::start` drops an image of another item or kind.
  - **Tests.** `tests/uv_coverage.rs::check_all_over_many_textures_holds_no_overlay_images` (40 textures: every answer cached, no `uv_overlay` for an item that is not expanded, then the expanded one's 512² image from a job) fails before and passes after. `check_all_polls_without_greying_advanced` and `the_ui_thread_never_reads_the_scan_db` now expect the image only after the job lands.
  - **Not measured.** The GUI's peak memory (record-mem.ps1) is still open, for CL42's reason: launching `tessalith.exe` reads and writes the user data folders.

Pinned in `tests/export.rs` through `AppModel::end_export_with` (a `#[doc(hidden)]` hook, because `WorkerMsg` is private); a mutation that drops the gate fails the cancel test.

The GUI saves it per file (`[files."up.png"] drop_top_mip = true`, `--set file:<glob>.drop_top_mip=true`), but like any profile field it is accepted at every scope (`[defaults]`, `[subtypes]`, presets, `--set`).

Schemas regenerated: `config`, `event`, `manifest`, `preset` and `profile` gain the optional field; `event` gains `low_detail` in `SkipKind` and the code; `dds-validation` the code.

The CLI honours a saved field.

With neither an export folder nor an MO2 instance the session cannot export and says "Export needs an export folder (Export to ▾) or an MO2 instance."

A shared alpha with a clip controller cannot be split in Phase A: "Shared alpha with a clip controller: splitting needs Phase C." Superseded by Note CR (Phase C splits clip-driven shared alphas).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CM, 2026-10-21)
- **CI28 Leaving the edit preview (final review I2).** "In game" still shows the real PGPatcher copy (CI11). While an edit is open, any view that is not the edit preview says so in one line, edits made meanwhile keep updating the session, and switching back re-loads the preview with every current edit. While editing, the shape cards, selection and "Show only this shape" come from the copy being edited, not from the copy the Meshes view shows (final review I1).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note GD5, 2026-10-29)
- **CI29 NIF export follows the Textures export folder (owner, 2026-10-20).** The NIF destination root is the Textures export folder (`AppModel::export_root`: the "Export to ▾" folder, else `<project root>\sdc-out`) whenever the Textures side has one (an export folder setting or an open project, as the header shows it). Otherwise it is the CI3 fallback `<instance>\mods\sdc NIF Edits`. The exported NIF is `<root>\<data path>` and the edit document `<root>\.sdc\nif-edits\<data path>.sdc-nifedit.toml`; CI27 and the guard and atomic-write rules are unchanged. The app hands the folder to the Meshes model every frame (one line in `app.rs`, owner-approved). When it changes while an edit is open, the session is saved to the old root (an unsaved drag too), then the same NIF is reopened from the new root: its edit document there if one exists, else a fresh one, with the selected shape kept when it still exists. The switch never replaces an edit document; the old one stays where it was (controller ruling, superseding P14's first re-point design). The placement check runs only when the root is a folder directly in the instance's `mods` (its folder name is the MO2 mod name); otherwise the line says "The export folder is not an MO2 mod here: load order not checked." and export still works. The panel shows "To: <full path>" (cut in the middle when long, full path on hover) and "Change it with Export to ▾ in the header." There is no NIF-only setting. This supersedes CI3's "always" and the plan's fixed-mod ruling (OD2) and "no edit_mod setting" (R3) only as far as the destination goes.

## Note CN — v2 Track A: PBR to Vanilla and Complex Material "Also export" (owner, 2026-10-20)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CV5, 2026-10-21)
- **CN17 The object job table, per-kind sources and picks (Task 4, TA8, TA13, TA29).**
  (amended by Note CV, item CV5)
  - **Vanilla:** `vanilla_diffuse` when there is an albedo; `legacy_n` when there is a `_n`; `env_m` when the metal slot is a real source (`ChannelSource::Source`, not a default or fill) and a scanned shape at the vanilla mesh key votes Environment Map. The mesh key is `vanilla_game_path(mesh_key(colour member's game path, its output stem without a §6.1 suffix))`. With metal but `scanned` false, the set gets `DERIVE-NO-SCAN`; with metal and no Environment Map vote, `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP`. No metal source gives neither.
  - **CM:** `cm_m`, plus `cm_diffuse` only when there is an albedo, `keep_metal_brightness` is on, metal is a real source and the strength is above 0.
  - **Slots each job holds:** colour maps only for the kinds that read them (albedo: diffuse kinds; normal: `legacy_n`); rough, metal and AO always; `_p` only for `cm_m` (the landscape cases are Task 5's); `_curves`/cavity only for the diffuse kinds. A kind that reads no colour map (`cm_m`, `env_m`) still lists the albedo (else the normal) first, with role `size`, so a thin set's constant `_m` has a size (TA22), and `input` is a real file. The cost: an albedo edit re-encodes the `_m`.
  - **Picks:** one per set that derived anything, `ShaderPick { shader, provenance: Derived, wanted: None }`. The shader is `Envmap` when the env `_m` is written, else `Default`, and `ComplexMaterial` in CM mode. A `Derived` pick raises no `SHADER-UNAVAILABLE` (`pick_notes`), and `check_maps` returns nothing for it. `ShaderOrigin::Derived` displays and parses as `derived`; the GUI's set card shows " (derived)".

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC3, 2026-10-29)
- **CN20 Set-level notes and the NIF suggestion (Task 4, TA11, TA16, TA28).** (superseded by FC3)
  - **Where the set-level notes go:** `DERIVE-NO-SCAN`, `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP`, `DERIVE-NO-ALBEDO` (when something is still derived; on the CM plan only when the TA8 CM diffuse would have been written, review M1) and `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED` ride on the set's diffuse job, else its first derived job.
  - **`DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED`:** one per member that fills no slot, with data `{member, reason}`. An RNM layer, by its name (`rnm::split_layer`), has the reason "detail layer not in the vanilla _n" (TA28).
  - **`DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST`:** data `{glossiness, specular_strength: 1.0, env_map}`, where `env_map` = the metal slot is a source. It is on the `_n` job, else the diffuse. The brief's code comment says "_n (or diffuse)", and its set-level list says the diffuse; the code comment is the more specific of the two. The glossiness is `nif_glossiness` of the plain mean of the assembled roughness slot (TA16). `assemble_derived_rough` returns that mean from the scalars it builds anyway, so nothing is decoded twice.
  - **Vanilla plan only:** `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED` and the NIF suggestion are raised on the Vanilla plan only, which every Also export writes (TA30), so the report lists them once.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CN42, 2026-10-20)
- **CN33 The copy rule (O4, TA25).** After the PBR run, each ready, non-derived, non-packed Vanilla job whose cs twin (a PBR-plan job with the same input) passes `also::same_identity` and is current is taken out of the Vanilla plan and copied from the PBR folder after the Vanilla run. `same_identity`: both `Ready`; equal inputs; equal `output_key`s relative to their roots, the cs key folded by `vanilla_rel` (O6's landscape `_p` copies); equal `pack`, `rnm` and `bake`; `van.derive` none; `Profile { target: Cs, ..van.profile } == cs.profile`. Current means the PBR folder's record passes `api::is_up_to_date` (now `pub(crate)`, not restated) for this run's twin, encoders, NVTT version and BC1 model, which also checks its file stamp. Any other twin is encoded, so a failed or stale PBR output is never copied and never leaves a gap. The copy record is the twin's record with `output` = the Vanilla key, `ts` = now, `output_stamp` after writing and `copied_from` = the twin's `output_stamp`. `ManifestRecord::copied_from` / `ManifestEntry::copied_from` is skipped when `None`, so no existing manifest line changes; only `manifest.schema.json` gains it. A copy is up to date when the Vanilla record's `sources` and `source_sha256` equal the twin's, its `copied_from` equals the twin file's stamp now, and its `output_stamp` matches the file (`FileSkipped UpToDate`); a changed BC1 model re-encodes the twin, its stamp moves, and the copy is redone. An existing file under `if-changed` goes through fix/pre-v2's `may_replace` with "the Vanilla record's `output_stamp` matches" as `ours`; `Kept` is `OUT-UNKNOWN-EXISTING` and the file is unchanged. `--on-existing skip` and `overwrite` act as for encodes. A copy ends with `FileDone` carrying the PBR record's validation. `shortcut:` a packed job is never copied (its raw sources' `packed_source` events belong to its encode), and a copy writes no `--sidecar`; upgrade if either matters. Copy outcomes count in the Vanilla phase's summary (up to date, skipped, failed, cancelled) and in `AlsoSummary::copied`, after that phase's `batch_done`, whose counts therefore leave them out. A dry run plans every phase and counts the copies, but writes and records nothing.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CN41, 2026-10-20)
- **CN38 Cancel and later errors.** `cancel` is checked between phases; a cancel, or an engine error of the Vanilla or CM phase, stops the later phases and sets `AlsoSummary::cancelled_after` to the last phase that ran (the error goes into `notes`). Errors before the PBR phase writes anything are returned as `Err`. `AlsoSummary::exit_code` is `combined().exit_code()`, but an export stopped before its last phase is at least 1 (partial) even when the phases that ran were clean.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC10, 2026-10-29)
- **CN43 A corrupt `also.json` is left alone (Task 7 review M4, M5).** `OwnedFiles::load` returns `io::Result`: a missing record is empty, but one that exists and cannot be read or parsed is an error, never read as empty. `run_also` then leaves the file exactly as it is (no rename, no rewrite), emits `ALSO-OWNED-UNREADABLE` and adds a note naming the file; Tasks 8 and 9 write no owned file (report, zips, sums) in that case. That is the safest choice: nothing it lists loses its ownership, so fixing or deleting the file is enough. Renaming it aside would make every listed file foreign at once. A missing record is created; an existing one is never rewritten by `run_also`. `OwnedFiles::write` returns `OwnedWriteError::{Kept, Io}`, and `Io`'s note reads "could not be written"; `Kept` (fix/pre-v2) is unchanged, so its exhaustive matches elsewhere are untouched. (superseded by FC10)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC12, 2026-10-29)
- **CN45 Zip export (C8, Task 8).** `engine::zip::write_zip(folder, files)` is an in-house writer (no new dependency): method 8 through `flate2::write::DeflateEncoder` (default level), CRC-32 from `flate2::Crc`, local headers with the sizes known up front (no data descriptor), flag 0x0800 (UTF-8 names), version 2.0, the central directory and the EOCD. Entry names are `<folder name>/<rel>` with `/`, sorted; the DOS time is fixed at 1980-01-01 00:00, so the same files zip to the same bytes. Checked with Windows `tar` and .NET `ZipFile`. TA17: no ZIP64; more than 65,535 entries, or any size or offset at or over 4 GiB - 1, is `ZipError::TooLarge`, and that zip is skipped with a note. `shortcut:` the archive is built in memory, so the 4 GiB ceiling is also a RAM ceiling (upgrade: a temp file plus rename). (superseded by FC12)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC3, 2026-10-29)
- **CN67 Files rows and Problems (re-check A5, P11).** Vanilla and CM rows carry the `PrefixSink` key `"<folder name>/…"`. `AppModel::also_file(key)` maps such a key to its file in the folders of the last Also export (`None` for any other key, which keeps `select_job`); Task 12's Problems click reveals it. `bottom.rs`'s File rows now skip warnings that do not count toward the badge, as item and set rows already did (owner answer 2), so a `DERIVE-*` Info is never a Problems row. No existing test pinned an Info File row. (superseded by FC3)

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note CN85, 2026-10-20)
- **CN75 Review shows the Also column only while a tick is on.** The brief said "when the target is cs or a tick is on"; the dispatch said the GUI must look exactly as before without a tick, apart from the menu items and the Advanced section. The column therefore needs a tick; Export to ▾ is where a first tick is set. Review opens on Export whenever a tick is on (CN61), so the column is there whenever it matters.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN33 The copy rule (O4, TA25).** After the PBR run, each ready, non-derived, non-packed Vanilla job whose cs twin (a PBR-plan job with the same input) passes `also::same_identity` and is current is taken out of the Vanilla plan and copied from the PBR folder before the Vanilla run (Note CN42). `same_identity`: both `Ready`; equal inputs; equal `output_key`s relative to their roots, the cs key folded by `vanilla_rel` (O6's landscape `_p` copies); equal `pack`, `rnm` and `bake`; `van.derive` none; `Profile { target: Cs, ..van.profile } == cs.profile`. Current means the PBR folder's record passes `api::is_up_to_date` (now `pub(crate)`, not restated) for this run's twin, encoders, NVTT version and BC1 model, which also checks its file stamp. Any other twin is encoded, so a failed or stale PBR output is never copied and never leaves a gap. The copy record is the twin's record with `output` = the Vanilla key, `ts` = now, `output_stamp` after writing and `copied_from` = the twin's `output_stamp`. `ManifestRecord::copied_from` / `ManifestEntry::copied_from` is skipped when `None`, so no existing manifest line changes; only `manifest.schema.json` gains it. A copy is up to date when the Vanilla record's `sources` and `source_sha256` equal the twin's, its `copied_from` equals the twin file's stamp now, and its `output_stamp` matches the file (`FileSkipped UpToDate`); a changed BC1 model re-encodes the twin, its stamp moves, and the copy is redone. An existing file under `if-changed` goes through fix/pre-v2's `may_replace` with "the Vanilla record's `output_stamp` matches" as `ours`; `Kept` is `OUT-UNKNOWN-EXISTING` and the file is unchanged. `--on-existing skip` and `overwrite` act as for encodes. A copy ends with `FileDone` carrying the PBR record's validation. `shortcut:` a packed job is never copied (its raw sources' `packed_source` events belong to its encode), and a copy writes no `--sidecar`; upgrade if either matters. Copy outcomes count in the Vanilla phase's summary (up to date, skipped, failed, cancelled) and in `AlsoSummary::copied`, after that phase's `batch_done`, whose counts therefore leave them out. A dry run plans every phase and counts the copies, but writes and records nothing.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN32 Also export: three folders, three runs (Task 7).** `engine::also::run_also` checks the location (CN36), then `ExportConfig::check()`, then the final folder names (CN37), and preflights all three requests before anything is written. The phases run in order: **PBR** (the request with `output_root` = `<location>/<name> PBR`, today's plan and run, events unprefixed so the GUI's PGPatcher-JSON rows match), **Vanilla** (target `vanilla`, `derive` mode Vanilla, the copy step of CN33), **CM** (mode Cm, only the derived items). Vanilla runs whenever `also` holds `vanilla` or `cm`, because CM installs on top of Vanilla (TA30); with `also = ["cm"]` the notes say "CM installs on top of Vanilla: the Vanilla folder is written too". With no PBR set to derive (none, or all skipped), the CM phase does not run, no CM folder is made, and the note is TA30's "no PBR sets: Vanilla holds copies only; no CM folder written". The project name is the first input folder's name. `run_also` never sets `bc1_model`: the caller's request carries it, so the PBR folder equals that user's plain export (O2, `also_export_bc1.rs`, which runs alone because the BC1 model is process-global). The CM phase keeps the jobs with `derive`, and the skipped, failed and cancelled entries of a `DeriveSet` member, so a non-PBR failure counts once (in Vanilla); its planning events are held and only those of the kept items are forwarded, so a CM row never waits for a terminal event that will not come.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN64 The worker and the end of an Also export (CN56).** `start_export_pass(fresh)` calls `worker::spawn_export_also` while a tick is on (a held export's second pass never does). It runs `run_also(session.engine(), session.cache(), ..)` under `spawn_guarded(.., "export", ..)`, so the `Panicked` router ends it, with `req.bc1_model = session.bc1_model()`. It sends `AlsoFinished(Box<AlsoSummary>)`, then `ExportDone`: `Err` when `run_also` failed or `AlsoSummary::error` names the PBR phase (CN56: `run_also` is `Ok` then; nothing is written after a failed PBR run, as after a failed plain export); otherwise `Ok(combined())` with `converted += copied`, because `batch_done` counts leave the O4 copies out. `AlsoFinished` becomes export notes (the summary's notes, the copies, the zips, a cancel after a phase, "Conversion report: <path>"), never a dialog; a Vanilla or CM phase error is an Error message, and that export's end line is logged as an Error too, while the PBR folder's JSON finishers still run (its run was complete).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN17 The object job table, per-kind sources and picks (Task 4, TA8, TA13, TA29).**
  - **Vanilla:** `vanilla_diffuse` when there is an albedo; `legacy_n` when there is a `_n`; `env_m` when the metal slot is a real source (`ChannelSource::Source`, not a default or fill) and a scanned shape at the vanilla mesh key votes Environment Map. The mesh key is `vanilla_game_path(mesh_key(colour member's game path, its output stem))`: the stem as written when the set has an albedo, without its §6.1 suffix when the anchor is the `_n` (Note CV5). With metal but `scanned` false, the set gets `DERIVE-NO-SCAN`; with metal and no Environment Map vote, `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP`. No metal source gives neither.
  - **CM:** `cm_m`, plus `cm_diffuse` only when there is an albedo, `keep_metal_brightness` is on, metal is a real source and the strength is above 0.
  - **Slots each job holds:** colour maps only for the kinds that read them (albedo: diffuse kinds; normal: `legacy_n`); rough, metal and AO always; `_p` only for `cm_m` (the landscape cases are Task 5's); `_curves`/cavity only for the diffuse kinds. A kind that reads no colour map (`cm_m`, `env_m`) still lists the albedo (else the normal) first, with role `size`, so a thin set's constant `_m` has a size (TA22), and `input` is a real file. The cost: an albedo edit re-encodes the `_m`.
  - **Picks:** one per set that derived anything, `ShaderPick { shader, provenance: Derived, wanted: None }`. The shader is `Envmap` when the env `_m` is written, else `Default`, and `ComplexMaterial` in CM mode. A `Derived` pick raises no `SHADER-UNAVAILABLE` (`pick_notes`), and `check_maps` returns nothing for it. `ShaderOrigin::Derived` displays and parses as `derived`; the GUI's set card shows " (derived)".

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN20 Set-level notes and the NIF suggestion (Task 4, TA11, TA16, TA28).**
  - **Where the set-level notes go:** `DERIVE-NO-SCAN`, `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP`, `DERIVE-NO-ALBEDO` (when something is still derived; on the CM plan only when the TA8 CM diffuse would have been written, review M1) and `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED` ride on the set's diffuse job, else its first derived job. In the GUI `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP` and `DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST` are Warning rows on the export's file rows, with **Open report** or **Show in Messages** (Note FC3).
  - **`DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED`:** one per member that fills no slot, with data `{member, reason}`. An RNM layer, by its name (`rnm::split_layer`), has the reason "detail layer not in the vanilla _n" (TA28).
  - **`DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST`:** data `{glossiness, specular_strength: 1.0, env_map}`, where `env_map` = the metal slot is a source. It is on the `_n` job, else the diffuse. The brief's code comment says "_n (or diffuse)", and its set-level list says the diffuse; the code comment is the more specific of the two. The glossiness is `nif_glossiness` of the plain mean of the assembled roughness slot (TA16). `assemble_derived_rough` returns that mean from the scalars it builds anyway, so nothing is decoded twice.
  - **Vanilla plan only:** `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED` and the NIF suggestion are raised on the Vanilla plan only, which every Also export writes (TA30), so the report lists them once.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN25 Codes and C6 (Task 4).** Seven codes, after `CHK-UV-GUTTER`: `DERIVE-DDS-SOURCE` and `DERIVE-NO-ALBEDO` (Warning), `DERIVE-NO-SCAN` and `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP` (Info), `DERIVE-PATH-CONFLICT` (Error), `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED` and `DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST` (Info). The count goes from 101 to 108, and `ALL.last()` is now `DeriveNifSuggest`; recount at merge. The schemas gain the seven strings; `ShaderOrigin` is a string in the schema, so `derived` adds no schema line. Without `derive` none of this runs (C6), and the golden runs are unchanged.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN26 Derive: landscape and grass (Task 5; O1, O3, O5, O6, TA5).**
  - **Which jobs:** by the set's PBR-phase pick (`DeriveSet::shader`). Under Vanilla, `pbr_landscape` gets the diffuse (`landscape_diffuse`) and the `_n` (`landscape_normal`); under CM, only the `_m` (R = B = 0, G = gloss with the floor, A = `_p` R clamped as CN16). `pbr_grass` gets the converted diffuse only under Vanilla (no `_n`, no env `_m`, no stacking, O3), and only the `grass_stack` (`complex_grass`) under CM. `pbr` is unchanged.
  - **Landscape has no metal (TA5):** no env `_m`, no `DERIVE-NO-SCAN`/`DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP`, and no CM diffuse even with Keep metal brightness. `DeriveJob::landscape` is set, so the recipe uses AO strength 0.3, ignores metal and multiplies the `_n` alpha by `landscape_spec` (2.5, O5) before saturation (CN8, CN10). The diffuse reads the set's height slot: its alpha is the `_p` R, opaque with no alpha channel when the set has no `_p`.
  - **O6, the kept `_p` (preflight P7):** under Vanilla, `plan_derived` keeps a derived landscape set's `_p` job (the `_p` member's own job, or the `Parallax` recipe job when the height is a raw `_height`) and folds its output and game path by TA12. The vanilla target never plans `Parallax`, so a Vanilla derive passes `plan_packing` the raw heights of its landscape sets (`derive_heights`). Those heights get the cs recipe `[h, R, R, opaque]` instead of `PACK-UNUSED-INPUT` (Task 5 review I1, ruling (a)). The set is empty without `derive`, so non-derive vanilla plans are unchanged (C6). In `run`, a file a recipe packs gets its `packed` event only, not a second `derived` one. It is kept only when the set is derived (no DDS member, an albedo or a `_n`). It resolves under the set's derived `Landscape` pick, so it raises no map notes (TA29). Under CM it is removed with the other members: the `_p` lives in the CM `_m` alpha and the Vanilla folder holds the file.
  - **Picks (TA29):** `Landscape` (vanilla landscape), `ComplexMaterial` (CM landscape), `Default` (vanilla grass, as the vanilla fallback of `pbr_grass` is today), `ComplexGrass` (the stack).
  - **Grass shape:** the stack is the albedo's width by twice its height. An albedo that is not square power-of-two fails with `GRASS-SHAPE` at resolve, as complex grass does today; nothing is resampled. Through `finish_derived` the stack keeps CN21's grass hook, so an opaque albedo plans `cutout`, not `remove`.
  - **NIF suggestion:** only `pbr` sets carry `DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST`. Landscape has no NIF (it is a TXST), and the vanilla grass diffuse has no `_n`, so a glossiness would be meaningless.
  - **`DERIVE-NO-ALBEDO`:** also noted under CM for a grass set, since the stack needs the albedo. `shortcut:` a grass set with an albedo but no `_n` writes no stack under CM (its members are `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED`); build the stack on a flat normal if such sets turn up.
  - **08e-1 (b):** PBR grass under `textures/landscape/` reaches the derive step as `pbr_grass` (GRAS evidence keyed by output stem), so it is derived as grass, not landscape.
  - **Tests** (`tests/derive_plan.rs`): `landscape_puts_p_in_the_diffuse_alpha_and_uses_ao_0_3`, `landscape_without_p_has_an_opaque_diffuse`, `grass_under_textures_landscape_is_still_grass`, `grass_is_a_plain_diffuse_under_vanilla_and_a_stack_under_cm`, `a_grass_stack_of_the_wrong_shape_is_grass_shape`, `a_landscape_height_becomes_the_vanilla_p`. The grass planner test forces `alpha.policy = remove` through `[files]`, so it fails without the CN21 hook.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN67 Files rows and Problems (re-check A5, P11).** Vanilla and CM rows carry the `PrefixSink` key `"<folder name>/…"`. `AppModel::also_file(key)` maps such a key to its file in the folders of the last Also export (`None` for any other key, which keeps `select_job`); Task 12's Problems click reveals it. Which File-row warnings are Problems rows follows the row table in `model/bottom.rs`: `DERIVE-NO-ENVMAP` and `DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST` are Warning rows with **Open report** or **Show in Messages** (Note FC3).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN42 Copies before the Vanilla run (Task 7 review M2; supersedes CN33's order).** `do_copies` runs before `engine.run(vplan)`, into the phase summary that `run`'s counts are then added to, so every copy event comes before the Vanilla `batch_done`. The copies read only the PBR folder and their keys are out of the Vanilla plan, so the order changes no byte. `batch_done`'s own counts still leave the copies out (they are in `AlsoSummary`'s Vanilla phase and `copied`); the GUI (Task 11) counts rows, not those numbers. The copy record keeps the twin's `profile`, `profile_sha256`, `preset` and `shader` (review L1, intended): `same_identity` already proved the bytes equal, and a later plain `--target vanilla` export into the Vanilla folder re-encodes the copies once, which is safe. `shortcut:` copies write no `--sidecar` (comment at `do_copies`).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN63 TA10 in the GUI.** `also_location()` is `[project] also_location` in `sdc-project.toml`, else `export_root()`; `also_folders()` joins it with `[export.names]` (default `<opened folder name> PBR/Vanilla/CM`, recomputed as the engine names them, pinned against `AlsoSummary::folders`); `also_root()` is the PBR folder whenever a tick is on, and `output_root()` is that or `export_root()`. `request()` (so `replan()` and the export) plans with `output_root()` and, while ticked, `skip_root = also_location()` (P15), so the plan on screen is the PBR phase's; `view_key` keys on `output_root()`. Re-check A4: `PendingJson.root` (as `PendingCs.root`/`PendingCm.root`) carries the root to `finish_json`, `pgp_record(root, path)` and `record_json_sha(root, ..)`; the three `prepare` functions and `cm_json::targets` (P13) use `output_root()`, so Review's "What will be written" and the JSON hash records follow the tick. P14: `export_stopped` checks the export folder only without a tick (Review's location row covers the rest); "Copy CLI command" passes `cli_output()` (the location while ticked); "Open export folder", the header's path, the DDS header patch and the JSON editor stay on `export_root()` (docs/gui.md says so). P19: `model::export::export_folder_problem` delegates to `engine::also::export_folder_problem`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN28 `[export]` in `sdc.toml` (Task 6; C3, C4, C2, TA9, TA10).** A project `sdc.toml` may hold `[export]`: `also` (`"vanilla"`, `"cm"`), `zip` (bool), `skip` (set ids, as `--set set:<id>` uses; the C2 opt-out), `[export.names]` (`pbr`, `vanilla`, `cm`) and `[export.legacy]` (`LegacyParams`, defaults k 0.9 and `landscape_spec` 2.5). Every table is `deny_unknown_fields`, so an unknown key is a parse error of `sdc.toml`. It is `ConfigFile::export: Option<ExportConfig>`, skipped when absent, so a config without it parses, and is written, as before (C6). The recipe settings are not `Profile` fields, so no profile hash moves (C4).

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN39 Owned files (TA21).** `also::OwnedFiles` keeps the hash records of the files written outside the manifests (Tasks 8 and 9: report, zips, sums) in `<PBR folder>/_sdc/also.json`, never at the location root, keyed like fix/pre-v2's JSON records (absolute, `/`-separated, lower-cased). `write` asks `may_replace` (the recorded or the pending SHA counts as ours), records the new SHA as pending and saves, writes with `write_atomic`, then moves pending to the record and saves; a crash between the rename and the save leaves a pending SHA that matches the file, so the next export still owns it. An I/O error comes back as `Kept::Unreadable`. Every non-dry run saves the record once after the PBR phase, so `also.json` is the one file an Also export adds beside a plain export's `_sdc`. Copies and owned files use `may_replace` and add no overwrite rule of their own. `AlsoSpec::inner_layer_sets` (TA27) is carried for the report (Task 9); `shortcut:` the CLI leaves it empty.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN43 A corrupt `also.json` is left alone (Task 7 review M4, M5).** `OwnedFiles::load` returns `io::Result`: a missing record is empty, but one that exists and cannot be read or parsed is an error, never read as empty. `run_also` then leaves the file exactly as it is (no rename, no rewrite), emits `ALSO-OWNED-UNREADABLE` and adds a note naming the file; Tasks 8 and 9 write no owned file (report, zips, sums) in that case. That is the safest choice: nothing it lists loses its ownership. In the GUI it is a Warning row with **Reset also.json**, which deletes only that unreadable record (Note FC10). Renaming it aside would make every listed file foreign at once. A missing record is created; an existing one is never rewritten by `run_also`. `OwnedFiles::write` returns `OwnedWriteError::{Kept, Io}`, and `Io`'s note reads "could not be written"; `Kept` (fix/pre-v2) is unchanged, so its exhaustive matches elsewhere are untouched.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN45 Zip export (C8, Task 8).** `engine::zip::write_zip(folder, files)` is an in-house writer (no new dependency): method 8 through `flate2::write::DeflateEncoder` (default level), CRC-32 from `flate2::Crc`, local headers with the sizes known up front (no data descriptor), flag 0x0800 (UTF-8 names), version 2.0, the central directory and the EOCD. Entry names are `<folder name>/<rel>` with `/`, sorted; the DOS time is fixed at 1980-01-01 00:00, so the same files zip to the same bytes. Checked with Windows `tar` and .NET `ZipFile`. TA17: no ZIP64; more than 65,535 entries, or any size or offset at or over 4 GiB - 1, is `ZipError::TooLarge`, and that zip is skipped with a note; in the GUI an Also export that would pass a limit is a Blockers row before it starts (Note FC12). `shortcut:` the archive is built in memory, so the 4 GiB ceiling is also a RAM ceiling (upgrade: a temp file plus rename).

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN46 What goes in a zip (TA23, P4).** `BatchSummary` holds counts only, so each phase's `Reporter` gets a `Recorder` as its first sink: it collects the unprefixed job keys of `FileDone` and `FileSkipped { UpToDate }`, which covers the Vanilla copies (they emit through the same reporter). After the phases, each folder that ran is walked, skipping its `_sdc` and any `.sdc-*.tmp`. A file whose `output_key` is in its phase's set is zipped under its on-disk name; every other file (a kept unknown file, a stale output such as an old CM diffuse after Keep metal brightness was turned off) gets the note `<folder>/<rel>: not zipped: not written by this export`. P3: Task 8's tests assert these notes and the summary's failure count; the report text is Task 9's.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN47 When zips are written.** `cfg.zip` applies after the phases, in `run_also` (the phases moved to `run_phases`, so every early return still reaches the zip step). No zip and no sums file when the cancel token is set or a phase returned `Err` (note `zip skipped: the export did not finish`), on a dry run, or when `also.json` cannot be read (CN43: `ALSO-OWNED-UNREADABLE` was raised already). File-level failures (a `GRASS-SHAPE`, a size mismatch) don't block the zips. Each folder that ran becomes `<location>/<folder name>.zip`: with `also = ["vanilla"]` that is two zips, and with no PBR sets the CM folder is not written, so not zipped (TA30).

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN48 Sums file (TA21).** `<location>/<project name> SHA256SUMS.txt` has one line per zip written this run, in phase order: `"{sha256}  {file name}"`, lower-case hex, two spaces, LF endings, as `tools/package-release.ps1` writes; the file is UTF-8, so it is ASCII unless a folder name is not. The zips and the sums file go through `OwnedFiles` (`may_replace` plus the pending SHA, then `write_atomic`): a zip Tessalith did not write is kept with its note and gets no sums line. With no zip written, no sums file is written.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN49 Zip failures are partial (Task 8 review M1, M3, M4, M5).** A zip or sums file not written (kept as foreign, `TooLarge`, a folder walk or write error) gets its note plus an `ALSO-ZIP-FAILED` (Warning) event, and sets `AlsoSummary::zip_failed`. `exit_code()` then raises 0 or 4 to 1, as a cancel does: the user asked for zips and did not get them all. When no zip is written but an older `<project> SHA256SUMS.txt` exists, it is left alone with a note saying it still lists an earlier export's zips. An owned record that cannot be read at the zip step also sets `zip_failed`. If the start check already reported it (CN43), it is not warned about again. If it broke since, it gets its own `ALSO-OWNED-UNREADABLE` warning and note, not a silent skip. The PBR reporter moved to `run_also`, so the zip step's warnings go out under the PBR batch, like the later phases' batch-level warnings. M4: the entry limit is now 65,534; 65,535 entries is `TooLarge` because 0xFFFF in the EOCD count is the ZIP64 sentinel. This tightens CN45's "more than 65,535". Tests pin the flags (0x0800), the DOS time (0) and the date (0x0021) in both headers, a UTF-8 name and the 65,534/65,535 boundary. 111 codes.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN50 Conversion report (C5, Task 9).** `engine::also::report::render(summary, rows, spec)` writes `<location>/<project name> conversion-report.md`: per-project file names (owner 2026-10-17, superseding C5's fixed names, TA21), in the location next to the three folders. It starts with `<!-- written by Tessalith; regenerated on every Also export -->`, then the folders that ran with converted, copied (Vanilla only), up-to-date, skipped and failed counts, "Cancelled after <phase>" or "Stopped: the <phase> phase failed: <message> (exit code N)", every `[export.legacy]` value (`toml::to_string`, field order), whether the mesh scan was read, one table row per converted PBR set (suggested NIF Glossiness `{:.1}` and Specular Strength 1 from `DERIVE-NIF-SUGGEST`, TA16; "Environment Map shader needed" yes/no; env `_m` written: yes / no: no scan / no: mesh not env-map / no for a set without metal; "-" for landscape and grass, which get no suggestion), each set's notes, the sets not converted with why, the CM install text and every `AlsoSummary::notes` entry verbatim (TA30's notes, "not zipped", zip failures, walk errors, `TooLarge`, the stale-sums note, an owned record that cannot be read). LF endings and no timestamp: the same inputs give the same bytes.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN51 When the report is written.** Last in `run_also`, after the zip step, so it carries the zip notes; after a cancel or a phase error too. Not on a dry run, and not when `ALSO-OWNED-UNREADABLE` was raised (CN43), as for the zips and the sums file. `run_also` now loads `OwnedFiles` once for both steps. It goes through `OwnedFiles::write` (`may_replace` plus the pending SHA, then `write_atomic`): a report Tessalith did not write is kept, its `Kept::note` goes into `AlsoSummary::notes`, and `AlsoSummary::report` stays `None`. Task 9 review (controller ruling), matching CN49: a report kept or not written (an I/O error) also raises `ALSO-REPORT-FAILED` (Warning) and sets `AlsoSummary::report_failed`, which raises an exit of 0 or 4 to 1 (partial). 112 codes.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN52 Where the rows come from.** Read from the plans before they run (`run` consumes them). The Vanilla plan gives everything: the NIF suggestion and the not-converted notes live there only (Task 4); its `DERIVE-*` job warnings, its error jobs' warnings and its load failures become set notes; a set it skipped whole (`DERIVE-DDS-SOURCE`, `DERIVE-NO-ALBEDO`) becomes a `SetNote` in `AlsoSummary::sets` (beside C2's skipped sets) and gets no row. The CM plan adds only its derived error jobs (`report::derive_error`: `recipe:<derive kind>` on a PBR-set member; a CM size mismatch or path conflict, prefixed `CM:`), never a plain error job the Vanilla phase reports already (review M2); its `DERIVE-NO-ALBEDO` and not-converted items would repeat the Vanilla ones. A member reported both as a set-level `DERIVE-NOT-CONVERTED` note and as `report_unread`'s per-file skip is listed once, keyed by its path, with the note's reason (it carries TA28's "detail layer not in the vanilla _n").

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN53 Per-set lines (TA26, TA27, P22).** "Toksvig not applied" for a set whose derived `LegacyN` `_n` in the Vanilla plan passes CL40's opt-in test, now `bake::plan::gloss_bake_opted` (extracted from `plan_gloss_bakes`, same test), when the CM folder is ticked. "inner-layer `_p` copied unchanged" for a set in `AlsoSpec::inner_layer_sets` (ids compared through `sets::fold_id`).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN54 The install line (P2).** `also::cm_lines(cfg)` is the one copy of the CM install text: "Install the CM folder on top of the Vanilla folder.", plus "It holds only the `_m` files." with Keep metal brightness off. The report prints it whenever the CM phase ran, with Keep metal brightness on or off; with only `vanilla` ticked, no PBR set (TA30), or a cancel or error before the CM phase, there is no CM folder, so it is left out (review M3). With no set rows after a cancel or error, the report says the export stopped before the Vanilla phase planned the sets, not "No PBR set was converted" (review M1). The GUI's Review calls the same function (Task 11).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN55 A CM-only derived error runs (Task 9 review I1, fixes Task 7).** `recipe_error_job` gives a derived output's plan-time error (`PACK-SIZE-MISMATCH`, `DERIVE-PATH-CONFLICT`) `derive: None`, so `run_phases`' `retain(derive.is_some())` dropped a CM-only one before the run: no `FileFailed`, no failed count, exit 0. The CM plan now also keeps a job that passes `report::derive_error`, so it is failed and counted in the CM phase (exit 1) with one terminal event; a plain error job stays out, as the Vanilla phase already counts it.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN56 A failed PBR run (Task 9 review N1).** The brief writes the report "even after a cancel or failure", so an `Err` from the PBR phase's `engine.run` is now recorded like a later phase's (`AlsoSummary::error` with `Phase::Pbr` and its exit code, plus `ALSO-PHASE-FAILED`) and `run_also` returns `Ok`; the report is then written when it can be (usually not: such an error is mostly the PBR folder itself, and the report's record lives there, so it gets `ALSO-REPORT-FAILED`). Errors before the PBR phase writes anything (location, names, preflight, the PBR plan) are still returned as `Err`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN57 CLI Also export (Task 10, C3).** `sdc convert` reads `[export]` through `Engine::export_config(&req)`. With `also` empty or absent, nothing changes: the same `plan`/`run`, events, output layout and exit codes. With `also` set, it calls `also::run_also` with `AlsoSpec { cfg, location: -o (default ./sdc-out), scanned: false, inner_layer_sets: {} }` and the same human/JSON sink (`tee_sink`, split out of `reporter`). `-o` is the location: the three folders `<input name> PBR/Vanilla/CM`, the report and the sums go in it.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN58 Exit code (Task 10, CN56).** `run_also` returns `Ok` even when the PBR phase failed, so the CLI exits with `AlsoSummary::exit_code()` and never infers it from `Err`: a failed PBR run exits 3 (`error` carries the code), a kept or failed zip or report is partial (1), a clean run is 0. An `Err` from `run_also` (location, `[export]` check, folder names, preflight such as a cs-only `--set`) goes through `EngineError::exit_code`: 2, before anything is written. Tests: `also_exit_codes_follow_the_summary_not_err`.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN59 No scan from the CLI (TA13, TA18, TA27).** The CLI reads no scan database: `scanned` is false, so the env `_m` says "no: no scan" in the report, and `pbr_grass` is never auto-picked (PBR grass 4.2, the same pre-existing gap). `inner_layer_sets` is empty (a `shortcut:` comment). The GUI passes the real values.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN64 The worker and the end of an Also export (CN56).** `start_export_pass(fresh)` calls `worker::spawn_export_also` while a tick is on (a held export's second pass never does). It runs `run_also(session.engine(), session.cache(), ..)` under `spawn_guarded(.., "export", ..)`, so the `Panicked` router ends it. It sends `AlsoFinished(Box<AlsoSummary>)`, then `ExportDone`: `Err` when `run_also` failed or `AlsoSummary::error` names the PBR phase (CN56: `run_also` is `Ok` then; nothing is written after a failed PBR run, as after a failed plain export); otherwise `Ok(combined())` with `converted += copied`, because `batch_done` counts leave the O4 copies out. `AlsoFinished` becomes export notes (the summary's notes, the copies, the zips, a cancel after a phase, "Conversion report: <path>"), never a dialog; a Vanilla or CM phase error is an Error message, and that export's end line is logged as an Error too, while the PBR folder's JSON finishers still run (its run was complete).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN68 Mesh votes and the spec (TA13, TA27, P21).** While a tick is on, `mesh_vote_keys()` (split out of `refresh_mesh_votes`) also asks for each colour member's vanilla key (`derive::vanilla_game_path`), so `textures/pbr/arm/steel` votes under `textures/arm/steel` too. `AlsoSpec::scanned` is `!mesh_votes.is_empty()`; `inner_layer_sets` are the ids of the sets whose `checks::height_neutral` is `White` (PBR multilayer with coat parallax).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)

One mod folder then holds the textures and the meshes, as a plain export does. CI29's root-change rule (save to the old root, reopen from the new one) covers a tick switched while an edit is open. NIF edits are never in the zips (TA23: a zip holds only this run's texture outputs). This is the only `app.rs` edit of Track A (the plan's "never edits app.rs" yields to the owner ruling); the NIF team's HANDOFF has the line.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN80 Review's Also column, top to bottom.** The two ticks; "Export location" cut in the middle (`header::elide_middle`, sized from the column), the full path on hover and in the AccessKit name ("Export location: {path}"), and "Choose…" (`PickerPurpose::AlsoLocation`, folder-scoped, refused while an export runs by `set_also_location`); the PBR, Vanilla and CM name fields (empty = the default, shown as the hint; committed on focus loss, Esc cancels; AccessKit "{PBR|Vanilla|CM} folder name"); with CM ticked, Keep metal brightness, its strength and the CM lines (`ui::review::also_cm_lines`: `engine::also::cm_lines` plus `CM_NEEDS_VANILLA` when only CM is ticked, backticks dropped on screen); "Also write a zip of each folder"; "Skip these sets"; the gate's Also reason in `status::RED` when there is one; and the link "Recipe settings are in Advanced", which opens the drawer. Nothing in the column says the export will succeed (Task 11 M5): it names the folders and the reason only.

ARCHIVED (superseded by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN93 Zip cancel and RAM (final review M1).** `zip::write_zip` takes the `CancelToken` and checks it before each entry; a cancel returns `ZipError::Cancelled`, so nothing is handed to `OwnedFiles::write`: no partial file, the old zip stays, and the zip gets the usual "not written: cancelled" note and `zip_failed` (CN49). Correction to CN45's `shortcut:`: peak RAM is about twice the zip size (the new zip in memory plus the old one `may_replace` reads to compare), near 8 GiB at the 4 GiB ceiling. No streaming rewrite (controller ruling).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN94 Files an earlier export left are always reported (final review I3, Task 13 M3; controller ruling).** Tessalith still never deletes them. Every Also export that finished (no cancel, no phase error) walks each folder that ran, zip on or off (`this_runs_files`, the walk TA23's zip used): a file that is not among the job keys this run wrote or found up to date (copies included) is kept and gets a note `<folder>/<rel>: not written by this export; kept as it is` (`NOT_WRITTEN`), or with the zip on the existing `not zipped: not written by this export` (`NOT_ZIPPED`), one note per file. The notes reach the report and the GUI's export notes. After a cancel or a phase error nothing is listed, since most files were not reached; "finished" is decided before the zips, so a cancel while zipping does not change it. `cm_lines(cfg, grass, stale)` now describes what the CM folder holds this run: with Keep metal brightness off "only the `_m` files", or "the `_m` files and the complex-grass stacks of the PBR grass sets" when a PBR grass set that is not skipped is exported (`grass`); and, in the report only, "It also holds files this export did not write, listed under Notes …" when the walk found any in the CM folder (`stale`). Review passes `stale = false` (it is shown before the export) and reads `grass` from the set views (`AppModel::also_grass`). Docs: gui.md, GUI-Guide, Texture-Types, Project-Config.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN95 The zips and the report come after the PBR folder's JSON (final review I2; controller ruling: the zip holds the finished PBR folder).** `run_also` is now `start_also` (the phases) then `finish_also(run, spec, extra, cancel)` (the walk of CN94, the zips, the sums and the report); `AlsoRun` carries the Recorder keys, the report rows, the project name, `grass`, `dry_run` and the PBR reporter across. `extra` lists files written into the PBR folder after the phases; they join the PBR folder's keys, so they are zipped and not noted (a path outside the PBR folder is ignored). The CLI calls `run_also`, i.e. `finish_also` with no extra file: it writes no PGPatcher, CS or CM JSON, so its PBR zip never holds one, and a JSON an earlier GUI export left is noted as not written by this export. GUI and CLI share the one `finish_also`, so the same files give the same bytes. The GUI worker sends `AlsoFinished(summary, sender)` and `ExportDone` after the phases, then waits on the sender; `finish_export` runs the JSON finishers, sends the paths they wrote (the PGPatcher JSON when `Finish::Written` or answered-in-Review overwrite; each CS PBR and CM file whose bytes on disk now equal the text this export made: written or found current), re-arms `export.running` with the export's token (Cancel reaches the zips, Export stays busy) and defers the close/open/error-dialog tail; the worker then runs `finish_also` and sends `AlsoZipped` with only the new notes, the zips and the report, which ends the export. Not passed: the recorded-SHA maps (an orphaned JSON has a record too) and the borrowed-slot copies (vanilla mode only; Also needs cs). A dropped sender (a panic, a new export) is no extra file, never a hang; a panic clears the sender and the deferred tail first, and a late `AlsoZipped` with no deferred tail is dropped. A PGPatcher JSON edited outside Tessalith whose overwrite question is answered after the export (`Dialog::OverwriteJson`) is written after the zip: it is not in it and its "not zipped" note stands. "Export finished" and its severity are decided at `ExportDone`, before the zips, so `AlsoZipped` adds one Warning message when a zip, the sums file or the report was not written. A cancel after the last phase (now a real window: the JSON step and the zips) skips the zips with `ZIP_SKIPPED` and sets `zip_failed`, so the export is partial (exit 1, as CN49); before, that window was too short to matter and exited 0.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN99 Also derives only from sets the PBR export converts (owner ruling 2026-10-21); CLI tests carried from Task 10 (M1-M3).** A PBR set with any member job still `Unresolved` or `Blocked` (an unacknowledged blocking code; together the GUI's "undecided", `ItemStatus::NeedsDecision`) is treated like a skipped set in `also::derive_sets`: its members go to `DeriveRequest::drop`, so Vanilla and CM get no derived file and no copy of it, and a `SetNote` "not derived: N maps undecided in the PBR export" goes to the report's "Sets not converted" and (new) the GUI's export notes, which now list every `SetNote`. Checked: in the default CLI flow the PBR folder's skips are exactly those jobs (5 = the steel set's 3 plus the cup set's 2), and a 24² grass set is `Blocked { DimNpot }` (so `derive_plan`'s GRASS-SHAPE test now acknowledges DIM-NPOT, and `also_export`'s zip-failure test fails a CM size mismatch instead). M1: a cancel on the Also path, through the engine's `SDC_TEST_CANCEL_ON` hook (it cancels the token `run_also` was given, as `cli_edges` does for the plain path): exit 1, no Vanilla or CM folder, a report that says it was cancelled. The console handler itself is not driven (no console in tests), as for the plain path. M3: `[export] zip = true` without `also` is a plain export (exit 0, no Also folder or zip); a malformed `[export]` (`zip = "yes"`) is a config error (exit 2, nothing written), as any bad `sdc.toml`. M2: with the default acceptance every file stays undecided, so the PBR folder gets no texture, the plain set is neither copied nor encoded in Vanilla, the PBR set is not derived (the ruling above, with its note), and the export is partial (exit 1). Before the ruling the derived diffuse, `_n` and `_m` were written from the undecided maps.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CN101 The overwrite question during the zip step (re-review N1).** Since CN95 the PGPatcher overwrite question can be open while an Also export's zips run (`running` re-armed). `request_close`, `request_open` (and so a dropped folder) and `resolve_open_unsaved` now answer a pending question "keep" first, with the "PGPatcher JSON kept as edited" note, also as a Warning message, before `ExportRunning` replaces the dialog: the same answer #44 gives at the export's end. The hand-edited file is never overwritten without an answer.

The CM phase's `batch_started.inputs` still counts every discovered file. Two codes added: `ALSO-PHASE-FAILED`, `ALSO-OWNED-UNREADABLE` (110 codes).

## Note CM — NIF v2 Phase B: animation clock, key editor, controllers, clips, In game preview (NIF team, 2026-10-21)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CM33, 2026-10-21)
- **CM12 Controllers tab (B-P32 D1-D15, B-P35).** Each property shows its controllers with a curve graph, a key table, an Interpolation combo (Linear or Quadratic), Add key and Remove key. The key cache is keyed by (controller, clip) and rebuilt in `reapply` through `nif::edit::keys_of`, never per frame. The tab has no Play controls (D3) and `curve()` returns a struct, not a Response (D4); both depart from the plan's B6 line. Times are never clamped between neighbours (D5). The axes freeze while a handle is dragged (D6). Tangent handles are the cubic Bézier equivalent of the Hermite tangents and move vertically only (D7). Linear to Quadratic keeps the shape; Quadratic to Linear drops the tangents (D8). Add key inserts at the cursor with the sampled value; a key already at that time disables it (D10; superseded by CM33, B-P43). TBC, XYZ and CONST keys are drawn but not edited (D11). Remove is disabled on a managed controller (D14). A drag is one undo step. Fix round (B-P35): a handle outside the graph cannot start a drag; tangent handles widen the view by at most half the curve's value span; a range that is not finite shows "These keys are out of range for the graph." and keeps the table; a flat range gets a span of max(|v| × 0.001, 0.5).

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note GD1, 2026-10-29)
- **CM26 B9: no-effect warnings (B-P39, B-P40).** Amber, on the controller's row, never blocking export: "This controller has no effect: Specular is off when the mesh loads." (glossiness LSCF 9, strength LSCF 10 or colour LSCC 0, non-PBR, SLSF1 bit 0 off; LSCF 9 since B-P41, CM32); "This controller has no effect: specular strength is 0 when the mesh loads." (the same three, Specular on, static strength exactly 0); "This controller has no effect: alpha test is off." (TestRef without the test bit); "Refraction strength has no visible effect in game." (LSCF 0); "Two controllers drive <name>: only the last one plays." (on each shader controller that does not play). Two TestRef controllers on one alpha get no warning: B9 tested duplicates on shaders only.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note GD6, 2026-10-29)
- **CM27 B9: an empty RMAOS slot is fully metallic.** On a PBR material with slot 5 empty, CS's default white RMAOS sets metallic to 1: the Albedo target is 0 on every pixel and the base colour goes to specular and reflectance. Reported to the owner. There is no guard or warning yet, and the preview's behaviour here was not checked.

## Note CP — BC7 weighting dropdown (owner, 2026-10-21)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note CP10, 2026-10-21)
- **CP6 GUI mapping.** Linear clears both fields; Perceptual sets `perceptual = true` and clears the weights; a weighting clears perceptual and sets the weights. The row's reset clears both. The row's value, provenance and "edited" mark come from whichever field decides it. Bulk skips a Perceptual pick on a texture where it is locked; Copy settings reads a texture's own `perceptual = true` as the row's "perceptual".

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FC8, 2026-10-29)
- **CP10 GUI writes (supersedes CP6's first sentence).** Perceptual: `perceptual = true`, weights cleared (unchanged). Linear: clears both when nothing is inherited; when an inherited perceptual or weighting is in effect, writes `perceptual = false` and `bc7_weights = "linear"` in the texture's own entry. A weighting: writes the weights, and `perceptual = false` when the inherited perceptual is true (else clears it). "Inherited" is read from the resolved profile when the texture has no own value, and from an own `false` / `"linear"` (an earlier override) when it has one. shortcut: an own weighting hand-written over a different inherited one is not seen through, so Linear clears it and the inherited value shows; a second Linear pick then overrides it. Likewise (re-review N1) an own `perceptual = true` over an inherited `true` reads as not inherited: Alpha ×8 → Perceptual → Linear clears it, the inherited Perceptual shows, and a second Linear pick overrides it. (superseded by FC8)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CP4 Backend/format rule.** The weights reach bc7e only. On a non-BC7 format or another backend (NVTT, rgbcx, ISPC BC6H) the field does nothing and the engine raises the same `ENC-WEIGHTS-IGNORED`; CLI and GUI both get it from the same resolve. The GUI also hides the row there ("BC7 only", "used only by the built-in BC7 encoder"), as the hidden-reason pattern does for other fields. The *Perceptual* option alone keeps the old row's lock (colour without data alpha, `FMT-PERCEPTUAL-DATA`); the weightings are open on any BC7 map, since data-alpha maps (`_n` with specular) are what they are for.

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)

ARCHIVED (wording before the edit; superseded in part by Note FM, 2026-11-02)
- **CP11 Fix round 1, other items.** The Advanced drawer locks the row (with ↺ kept) on non-BC7 formats and non-bc7e backends, with the hidden reasons (I2). Changing a texture's format away from BC7 clears its own `bc7_weights` (M1). The unit-weights proof covers every level and both exact-opaque settings (M2). An invalid value such as `alpha4` is refused, naming the key and the valid values (M3). Both combos are named "BC7 weighting" for AccessKit (M4). `Bc7Weights::ALL` is tied to the enum by a schema test, and the engine test and the kittest read their options from it.
